# Aramaic Targum to Psalms

תרגום תהלים

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms)

תרגומים על ספרי כתובים במגוון סגנונות ממגוון תקופות.

Targumim on the books of Writings, ranging in style and date of composition.

Sections: Chapter-Verse

Location: Tanakh/Targum/Aramaic Targum/Writings

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): Mikraot Gedolot (Segments: 2527, License: Public Domain)

Version (En): The Psalms Targum: An English Translation (Edward M. Cook) (Segments: 481)


## Chapter 1

###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/1:1)


טוּבֵיהּ דִגְבַר דְלָא הַלִיךְ בְּמִלְכַּת רַשִׁיעִין וּבְאוֹרְחַת חַיָבִין לָא קָם וּבְסִיעַת מְמִקְנֵי לָא אִסְתְּחַר:


Happy the man who has not walked in the council of the wicked, or stood in the paths of sinners, or taken a seat in the band of mockers.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/1:2)


אֱלָהֵן בְּנִימוּסָא דַייָ רְעוּתֵיהּ וּבְאוֹרַיְתֵיהּ מְרַנֵן יֵמָם וְלֵילֵי:


Instead his pleasure is in the law of the Lord, and in his Torah he meditates day and night.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/1:3)


וִיהֵי כְּאִילָן חַיֵי דִנְצִיב עַל טְיָפֵי מוֹי דִי אִנְבֵּיהּ מְבַשֵׁל בְּעִדָנֵיהּ וְאַטְרְפוֹי לָא נָתְרִין וְכָל לַבְלְבוֹי דִמְלַבְלֵב מְגַרְגָר וּמַצְלָח:


And he will be like a living tree planted by streams of water, whose fruit ripens in due course, and its leaves do not fall, and all its branches that grow ripen and flourish.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/1:4)


לָא מְטוּל הֵיכְנָא רַשִׁיעֵי אֱלָהֵן כְּמוֹזָא דִי תַשְׁקְפִנֵיהּ עַלְעוֹלָא:


Not so the wicked; instead, they are like the chaff that the storm-wind will drive.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/1:5)


מְטוּל הֵיכְנָא לָא יִזְכּוּן רַשִׁיעֵי בְּיוֹמָא רַבָּא וְחַיָבִין בְּסִיעַת צַדִיקַיָא:


Therefore the wicked will not be acquitted in the great day, nor sinners in the band of the righteous,


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/1:6)


מְטוּל דִגְלֵי קֳדָם יְיָ אוֹרַח צַדִיקַיָא וְאוֹרְחַתְהוֹן דְרַשִׁיעֵי תְהוֹבֵד:


Because the path of the righteous is manifest in the Lord’s presence, but the paths of the wicked will perish.



## Chapter 2

###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/2:1)


לְמָה מִתְרַגְשִׁין עַמְמַיָא וְאוּמַיָא מְרַנְנִין סְרִיקוּתָא:


Why are the nations disturbed, and murmuring vanity?


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/2:2)


קָיְמִין מַלְכֵי אַרְעָא וְשִׁלְטוֹנַיָא יִתְחַבְּרוּן כַּחֲדָא לְמָרָדָא קֳדָם יְיָ וּלְמִנְצֵי עַל מְשִׁיחֵיהּ:


The kings of the earth arise and the rulers will join together to rebel in the Lord’s presence, and to strive against His Anointed.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/2:3)


נִתְרַע יַת אֱסָרָתֵיהוֹן וְנִטְלוֹק מִנָנָא שַׁלְשְׁלָוָתֵהוֹן:


They say, “Let us break their bonds, and let us throw off their chains from us.”


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/2:4)


דְיָתֵיב בִּשְׁמַיָא יִגְחַךְ מֵימְרָא דַייָ יִדְהַךְ לְהוֹן:


The One Who sits in Heaven will laugh; the word of the Lord will mock at them.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/2:5)


הֵידֵין יְמַלֵל לְהוֹן בְּתוּקְפֵיהּ וּבְרוּגְזֵיהּ יְבַהַלְאִנוּן:


Then He will speak to them in His strength, and in His wrath He will frighten them.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/2:6)


וַאֲנָא רַבִּיתִי מַלְכִּי וּמַנִיתֵיהּ עַל טוּר מַקְדְשִׁי:


"And I have anointed My king, and appointed him over My Sanctuary."


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/2:7)


אֶשְׁתְּעֵי קְיָמָא דַייָ אֲמַר חֲבִיב כְּבַר לְאַבָּא לִי אַנְתְּ זַכָּאָה כְּאִלוּ יוֹמָא דֵין בְּרֵיתָךְ:


I will tell of the covenant of the Lord. He said: “You are as dear to Me as a son to a father, pure as if this day I had created you.”


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/2:8)


בְעֵי מִנִי וְאֶתֵּן נִכְסֵי עַמְמַיָא אַחְסַנְתָּךְ וַאֲחוּדָתָךְ שִׁלְטוֹנֵי סְיָפֵי אַרְעָא:


"Ask Me and I will give the spoils of nations as your inheritance, the rulers of the ends of the earth as your holding."


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/2:9)


תְּתַבְּרִנוּן הֵיךְ בְּחוּטְרָא דְפַרְזְלָא הֵיךְ מָאן דִפְחַר תִּתְרְעִנוּן:


"You will shatter them as with a rod of iron, like a potter’s vessel you will break them."


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/2:10)


וּכְדֵין מַלְכַיָא אַשְׂכִּלוּ קַבִּילוּ מַרְדוּתָא נְגִידֵי אַרְעָא:


And now, O kings, grow wise; accept discipline, O princes of the earth.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 2:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/2:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/2:11)


פְּלָחוּ קֳדָם יְיָ בְּדַחֲלָא וְצַלוּ בִּרְתִיתָא:


Worship in the presence of the Lord with fear, and pray with trembling.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 2:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/2:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/2:12)


קַבִּילוּ אוּלְפָנָא דִלְמָא יִרְגֵיז וּתְהוֹבְדוּן אוּרְחָא מְטוּל דְיֵיחוֹר כִּזְעֵר רוּגְזֵיהּ טוּבֵיהּ לְכָל דְסָבְרִין בְּמֵימְרֵיהּ:


Accept instruction lest He be angry, and you lose your way; for His wrath will tarry a little. Happy all who trust in His Word!



## Chapter 3

###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/3:1)


תֻּשְׁבַּחְתָּא לְדָוִד בְּמַעַרְקֵיהּ מִן קֳדָם אַבְשָׁלוֹם בְּרֵיהּ:


A psalm of David, when he fled from the presence of Absalom his son.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/3:2)


יְיָ כַּמָה סַגִיאִין מְעִיקַי סַגִיאִין דְקַיְמִין עָלַי:


Lord, how many are my oppressors, many who arise against me!


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/3:3)


סַגִיאִין אָמְרִין לְנַפְשִׁי לֵית פּוּרְקָנָא לֵיהּ בֵּאלָהָא לְעָלְמִין:


Many say to my soul, “There is no redemption for him in God forever.”


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/3:4)


וְאַנְתְּ יְיָ תְּרִיס עֲלֵי יְקָרִי וּמְרִים רֵישִׁי:


But you, Lord, are a shield over me, my glory and the one who raises my head.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/3:5)


קָלִי קֳדָם יְיָ אֲצַלֵי יְקַבֵּל צְלוֹתִי מִן טוּר בֵּית מַקְדְשֵׁיהּ לְעָלְמִין:


I pray, my voice in the presence of the Lord; He will accept my prayer from the Mount of His Holy Temple forever.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/3:6)


אֲנָא שְׁכִיבֵית וּדְמַכֵית אִתְעֲרֵית מְטוּל דַיְיָ סָמִיךְ יָתִי:


I lay down, and I slept; I awoke, because the Lord sustains me.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/3:7)


לָא תִדְחַל מִמַצוּתָא דְעָם דַחֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר אִתְכְּנַשׁוּ עֲלָי:


I will not be afraid of the myriads of people who have gathered against me all around.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/3:8)


קוּם יְיָ פְּרוֹק יָתִי אֱלֹהָי אֲרוּם מַחֵיתָ כָּל בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבַי עַל לִיסַתְהוֹן כַּכֵי רַשִׁיעֵי תִּבַּרְתָּ:


Arise, O Lord, redeem me, O my God; for you have struck all my enemies on their cheek, you have broken the teeth of the wicked.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/3:9)


מִן קֳדָם יְיָ פּוּרְקָנָא עַל עַמָךְ בִּרְכָתָךְ לְעָלְמִין:


Redemption is from the presence of the Lord; your blessings are to your people forever.



## Chapter 4

###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/4:1)


לְשַׁבָּחָא עַל חִנְגִיתָא תּוּשְׁבַּחְתָּא לְדָוִד:


For praise, with melodies: A hymn of David


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/4:2)


בְּעִדָן צְלוֹתִי קַבֵּל מִנִי אֱלוֹהַּ צִדְקוֹתִי בְּעִידָן עַקְתָא אַפְתִּיתָא לִי חוּס עֲלַי וְקַבֵּל צְלוֹתִי:


At the time of my prayer, accept it from me, O God of my righteousness; at the time of distress, you relieved me; pity me and accept my prayer.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/4:3)


בְּנֵי נְשָׁא מְטוּל מֶה אַיְקָרִי לְאִתְכְּנָעוּתָא תְּרַחֲמִין סְרִקוּתָא תִּבְעוּן כַּדִיבוּתָא לְעָלְמִין:


Sons of men, why is my glory for humiliation? You will love vanity; you will seek falsehood forever.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/4:4)


וִידַעוּן אֲרוּם פָּרִישׁ יְיָ זַכָּאָה לֵיהּ יְיָ יְקַבֵּל צְלוֹתֵיהּ דְדָוִד בְּמִקְרֵי לְוָתֵיהּ:


And they will know, for the Lord has separated the righteous man for Himself; the Lord will accept the prayer of David when he calls to Him.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/4:5)


זְעוּ מִנֵיהּ וְלָא תֶחֱטוּן אִמְרֵי בָעוּתְכוֹן בְפוּמְכוֹן וְשֵׁילַתְכוֹן בִּלְבַבְכוֹן וְצַלוּ עַל שִׁווּיֵכוֹן וְאִדְכַּרוּ יוֹם מִיתוּתָא לְעָלְמִין:


Tremble for Him, and do not sin; utter your petition with your mouth and your request in your heart; and pray upon your beds and remember the days of death forever.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/4:6)


כְּבַשׁוּ יִצְרְכוֹן וְיִתְחַשֵׁיב לְכוֹן כְּנִכְסַת צִדְקָא וְסַבָּרוּ עַל יְיָ:


Subdue your impulses and it will be reckoned to you as a righteous sacrifice; and hope in the Lord.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/4:7)


סַגִיאִין דְאָמְרִין מַן יַחֲמִינָנָא טָבָא נְשָׂא עֲלָנָא נְהוֹר סְבַר אַפָּךְ יְיָ:


Many say, “Who will show us good?” Lift on us the light of your countenance, O Lord


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/4:8)


יְהֵבְתָּא חֶדְוָתָא בְלִבִּי מִן עִדָן דְעִבּוּרְהוֹן וְחַמְרֵהוֹן סַגִיאוּ:


You have placed joy in my heart when their grain and their wine has increased.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/4:9)


בִּשְׁלָמָא כַּחֲדָא אֶשְׁכּוֹב וְאֶדְמוֹךְ מִן בִּגְלַל דְאַנְתְּ הוּא יְיָ בִּלְחוֹדוֹי בְּסִבְרָא תּוֹתְבִנַנִי:


In peace I both lay down and sleep, because you alone are the Lord; in security you will make me dwell.

For praise, with dancing. A hymn of David.

צֵית לְקָל בָּעוּתִי מַלְכִּי וֵאלָהִי אֲרוּם קֳדָמָךְ אֲצַלִי:


Hear the sound of my petition, my king and God, for I will pray in your presence.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/5:4)


יְיָ בְּצַפְרָא שְׁמַע קָלִי בְּצַפְרָא אֲסַדֵר קֲדָמָךְ וְאֶסְתְּכֵי:


O LORD, in the morning hear my voice; in the morning I set myself before you and keep watch.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/5:5)


מְטוּל דְלָא אֱלָהָא רָעֵי רִשְׁעָא אַנְתְּ לָא אִתּוֹתַב עִמָךְ בִּישָׁא:


Because you are not a God who takes pleasure in¹³ wickedness; evil did not abide with you.

¹³ does not desire


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/5:6)


לָא יִתְעַתְּדוּן מְתַלְעֲבִין קֳבֵל עַיְנָךְ סְנֵיתָא כָּל עוֹבְדֵי שְׁקָר:


Scoffers shall not stand before your eyes; you hate all deeds¹⁴ of falsehood.

¹⁴ doers

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/5:7)


תְּאַבֵּד מְמַלְלֵי כַדְבוּתָא אֱנָשׁ שָׁדֵי דַם זַכַּי וְנִכְלָא יְרַחֵק יְיָ:


You will destroy tellers of lies; the Lord will loath the man *who sheds innocent* blood and the deceiver.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/5:8)


וַאֲנָא בִּסְגִיאֵי טוּבָךְ אֵעוּל לְבֵיתָךְ אֶסְגוֹד לְהֵיכְלָא דְקוּדְשָׁךְ בִּדְחַלְתָּךְ:


And I, through your great goodness, will enter your house; I will bow down to your holy temple in awe of you.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/5:9)


יְיָ דַבֵּר יָתִי בְּצִדְקְתָךְ מִן בִּגְלַל תּוּשְׁבְּחִי תַּקִין קֳדָמַי אוֹרְחֲתָךְ:


O LORD, guide me by your righteousness; because of my *hymn*, make firm your paths before me.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/5:10)


מְטוּל דְלֵית בְּפוּמְהוֹן דְרַשִׁיעֵי כֵיוַנְתָּא גוּפֵיהוֹן מַלְיָן אִתְרְגוּשְׁתָּא הֵיךְ שְׁיוֹל פְתִיחַ גְרוֹנְהוֹן לִישַׁנְהוֹן מְשָׁעֲעִין:


Because there is no reliability in the mouths *of the wicked;* their bodies *are full of* tumult; *like Sheol,* their throat is open; their tongues flatter.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 5:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/5:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/5:11)


חַיֵב לְהוֹן אֱלָהָא יִתְבַּטְלוּן מִמִלְכָּתְהוֹן בְּסִגְעָא מְרָדֵהוֹן אַתְקַל לְהוֹן אֲרוּם מְרַדוּ בְמֵימְרָךְ:


God has accused them; they will be done away with by their counsel; for their great sin he overturned them, for they rebelled against *your command.*


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 5:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/5:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/5:12)


וְיֶחְדוּן כָּל מְסַבְּרוּן בְמֵימְרָךְ לַעֲלַם יְשַׁבְּחוּן וְתַטֵל עֲלַוֵיוֹן וְיִבְדְחוּן בָךְ רַחֲמֵי שְׁמָךְ:


And all who trust in your word will rejoice forever; they will give praise and you will protect them; and those who love your name will rejoice in you.¹⁵

¹⁵ in your word

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/5:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/5:13)


מְטוּל דִי אַנְתְּ תְּבָרֵךְ לְצַדִיקַיָא יְיָ הֵיךְ תְּרֵיסָא רַעֲוָא טָבָא תְּכַלְלִנֵיהּ:


Because you will bless the righteous, O LORD; as with a shield, you will crown him with *good* will.

לְשַׁבָּחָא בִּנְגִינָתָא עַל כִּנָרָא דִתְמַנְיָא נֵימַיָא תּוּשְׁבַּחְתָּא לְדָוִד:


For praise; with melodies on *the harp of eight strings*. A hymn of David.

יְיָ לָא בְּרוּגְזָךְ תַּכְנִיעֵנִי וְלָא בְּרִתְחָךְ תִּרְדֵי יָתִי:


O LORD, do not humble¹⁶ me in your anger; and do not punish me in your wrath.

¹⁶ rebuke


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/6:3)


חוּס עֲלַי יְיָ מְטוּל דְחַלָשׁ אֲנָא אַסִי יָתִי יְיָ מְטוּל דְאִתְבְּהִילוּ גַרְמָי:


Pity me, O LORD,¹⁷ for I am weak; heal me, O LORD, for my bones are terrified.

וְנַפְשִׁי אִתְבְּהִילַת לַחֲדָא וְאַנְתְּ יְיָ עַד אֵימָתַי רְוַח לִי:


And my soul is greatly terrified; and you, O LORD, when *will you give me relief*?

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/6:5)


תּוּב יְיָ פְּצֵי נַפְשִׁי פְּרַק לִי בִּגְלַל טוּבָךְ:


Turn, O LORD, save my soul, redeem me for the sake of your goodness.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/6:6)


מְטוּל דְלֵית בְּמוֹתָא דָכְרָנָךְ בִּשְׁאוֹל מַן יוֹדֵי לָךְ:


For there is no memory of you in death; in Sheol who will give you thanks?


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/6:7)


אֶשְׁתַּלְהֵית בְּתִנְחָתִי אֲמַלֵל בְּצַעֲרִי בְּכָל לֵילְיָא עַל שִׁווּיִי בְדִמְעֲתִי דַרְגָשִׁי אַטְמֵשׁ:


I am wearied with my groaning; I will *speak¹⁸ in my sorrow* every night on my bed; I will drown my couch with my tears.

¹⁸ Tg אמלל suggests אשיחה

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/6:8)


חַשְׁכַת מִן רוּגְזִי עַיְינַי אִתְבְּלִיאַת בְּכָל מְעִיקָי:


My eye is dark from my trouble; it is worn out by all my oppressors.¹⁹

footnotes:
¹⁹ distress


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 6:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/6:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/6:9)


זוּרוּ מִינִי כָּל עָבְדֵי שְׁקַר אֲרוּם שְׁמִיעַ קֳדָם יְיָ קָל בָּכוּתִי:


Leave me, all doers of falsehood; for the LORD has heard²⁰ the sound of my weeping.

footnotes:
²⁰ accepted


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 6:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/6:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/6:10)


שְׁמִיעַ קֳדָם יְיָ בָּעוּתִי יְיָ צְלוֹתִי יְקַבֵּל:


My petition²¹ has been heard in the LORD’s presence; the LORD will accept my prayer.

footnotes:
²¹ supplication


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 6:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/6:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/6:11)


יִבְהֲתוּן וְיִתְבַּהֲלוּן לַחֲדָא כּוּלְהוֹן בַעֲלֵי דְבָבִי יְתוּבוּן יִבְהֲתוֹן כְּשָׁעָא:


All of my enemies will be ashamed and very afraid; they will turn and be ashamed in an instant.



## Chapter 7

###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/7:1)


תַּרְגוּמָא דְאוֹדֵיתָא לְדָוִד דִי שַׁבַּח קֳדָם יְיָ מְטוּל דִי אֲמַר שִׁירָתָא עַל תְּבִירָא דְשָׁאוּל בַּר קִישׁ דְמִן שֵׁבֶט בִּנְיָמִן:


*A rendition of the thanksgiving* of David, who gave praise to the Lord; for he spoke *a song about the ruin of Saul son of Kish, from the tribe of* Benjamin.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/7:2)


יְיָ אֱלֹהַי בְּמֵימְרָךְ סַבְּרִית פְּרוֹק יָתִי מִכָּל רָדְפַי וּפְצָא יָתִי:


O LORD my God, I have trusted in *your word;* deliver me from all my persecutors and save me.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/7:3)


דִלְמָא יִתַבַּר הֵיךְ אַרְיְוָתָא נַפְשִׁי יִפְשַׁח וְלֵית דְיִפְצֵי:


Lest he crush my soul like a lion; he will tear and there is no one who will deliver *me*.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/7:4)


יְיָ אֱלֹהַי אִין עֲבַדֵית שִׁירָתָא הָדָא בְּכַוַונְתָּא בִישָּׁא אִין אִית טְלוּמָא בִּידָי:


O LORD my God, if I have made this *song with evil intent*, if there is oppression in my hand,


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/7:5)


אִין פַּרְעִית לִבְעֵיל שְׁלָמִי בִּישׁ וּדְחַקֵית מְעִיקַי מַגָן:


If I have repaid my ally with evil, or thrust away my oppressor for nothing,


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/7:6)


יְהֵי רָדֵף בְּעֵל דְבָבָא נַפְשִׁי וְיַדְבֵּק וְיִכְבּוֹשׁ לְאַרְעָא חַיַי וִיקָרִי לְעַפְרָא יַשְׁרֵי לְעָלְמִין:


May the enemy pursue my soul, and may he overtake and trample my life to the ground, and may my honor rest in the dust forever.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/7:7)


קוּם יְיָ בְּתוּקְפָךְ אִתְנַטַל בְּרוּגְזָא עַל מְעִיקַי וְסַרְהֵיב לִי דִינָא דִי פַקֵדְתָּא:


Arise, O LORD, in your might; be lifted up in anger *against* my oppressors; and bring hastily to me the justice that you commanded.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/7:8)


וּכְנִישַׁת אֻמַיָא תַּחְזְרִינָךְ וְאַמְטֻלְתָּהָא לְבֵי שְׁכִנְתָּךְ תּוּב:


The gathering of the nations will surround you; because of it return to *the place of your presence.*


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/7:9)


מֵימְרָא דַייָ יְדִין עַמְמַיָא דוֹן יָתִי יְיָ כִּזְכוּתִי וּכְשַׁלְמוּתִי פְּרַע עֲלָי:


*The word of* the LORD will judge the Gentiles; judge me, O LORD, by my merit, and for my innocence recompense me.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 7:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/7:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/7:10)


יִשְׁתֵּצֵי כְעַן בִּישָׁא דְרַשִׁיעֵי וְיִשְׁתַּכְלְלוּן צַדִיקֵי וּבָחֵן לִבְּבֵי וְכָלְיָן אֱלָהָא זַכָּאָה:


Now may the evil of the wicked perish; and let the righteous *be firmly established*; and the righteous God examines hearts and minds.²²

footnotes:
²² lit. kidneys


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 7:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/7:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/7:11)


תְּרֵיסִי עַל אֱלָהָא פָּרִיק תְּרִיצֵי לִבָּא:


My shield is on God, redeemer of the upright of heart.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 7:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/7:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/7:12)


אֱלָהָא דַיָנָא זַכָּאָה וּבִתְקוֹף רָגִיז עַל רַשִׁיעֵי כָּל יוֹמָא:


God is the righteous judge, and *in might* is angry *at the wicked* every day.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 7:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/7:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/7:13)


אִם לָא יְתוּב לִדְחַלְתֵּיהּ סַיְפֵיהּ שְׁחִיז קַשְׁתֵּיהּ מְתִיחָא וּסְדִירָא:


If one does not repent *and reverence him*,²³ his sword is whetted, his bow drawn and ready.

footnotes:
²³ lit., return to his fear


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 7:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/7:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/7:14)


וּמְטוּלְתֵּיהּ תַּקֵין זְיָנֵי מוֹתָא גִירוֹי לְדָלְקֵי צַדִיקַיָא יַעְבֵּיד:


On his account, he has prepared the weapons of death; he will make his arrows for those who pursue *the righteous*.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 7:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/7:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/7:15)


הָא יִצְטַעַר לִשְׁקָר וִיעֵדֵי עַמְלָא וְיֵלֵיד שִׁקְרָא:


Behold, he will be in pains with falsehood, and will conceive trouble, and give birth to falsehood.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 7:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/7:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/7:16)


שׁוּחַ חֲפַר וְנַמְצֵיהּ וּנְפַל בְּשׁוּחֲתָא דִי עֲבָד:


He has dug a pit and deepened it; and he fell in the pit he made.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 7:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/7:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/7:17)


יְתוּב לֵעוּתֵיהּ בְּרֵישֵׁיהּ וְעַל מוֹקְדֵיהּ חֲטוֹפֵיהּ יֵחוֹת:


His misery will return on his head; and on his pate his rapacity will descend.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 7:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/7:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/7:18)


אֲשַׁבַּח יְיָ בְּצִדְקְתֵיהּ וַאֲשַׁכַּח שׁוּם אֱלָהָא עִלָאָה:


I will thank²⁴ the LORD according to his righteousness; and I will praise the name of God Most High.

footnotes:
²⁴ give praise



## Chapter 8

###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/8:1)


לְשַׁבָּחָא עַל כִּנוֹרָא דְאַיְתֵי מִגַת תּוּשְׁבַּחְתָּא לְדָוִד:


For praise, *on the lyre that he brought from* Gath. A hymn of David.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/8:2)


יְיָ אֱלָהָא רִבּוֹנָנָא כְּמָא תְלִיל שְׁמָךְ וּמְשַׁבַּח בְּכוֹלָא אַרְעָא דִיהַבְתָּא זִוָךְ עֵיל מִן שְׁמַיָא:


O God²⁵ our master, how lofty is your name *and praiseworthy* in all the earth, you who have placed your splendor above the heavens.

footnotes:
²⁵ LORD


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/8:3)


מִפּוּם עוּלֵמַיָא וְיוֹנְקַיָא אִשְׁתֵּסְתָּא עוֹשָׁנָא מִן בִּגְלַל מְעִיקָיִךְ לְבַטָלָא בְּעֵיל דְבָבָא וְגָזוֹמָא:


From the mouth of children and infants you have established strength because of your oppressors, to bring to naught the enemy and the violent man.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/8:4)


מְטוּל דְאֶחְמֵי שְׁמָיִךְ עוֹבָדִי אֶצְבְּעָתָךְ סִיהֲרָא וְכֹכְבַיָא דִי אַתְקֵינְתָּא:


Because I see your heavens, the works of your fingers, the moon and the stars that you have fixed in place,


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/8:5)


מַה בַּר נָשָׁא מִטוּל תִּדְכַּר עוֹבָדוֹי וּבַר נָשָׁא מְטוּל תִּסְעַר עֲלוֹי:


What is a son of man, because you will remember *his deeds*, and a son of man, because you will punish him?


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/8:6)


וְחִסַרְתָּא יָתֵיהּ קַלִיל מִמַלְאֲכַיָא וְאַיְקָרָא וְשִׁבְהוֹרָא תֵכְלְלִנֵיהּ:


And you have made him a little less than *the angels*, and you will crown him with glory and brightness.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/8:7)


אַשְׁלַטְתֵּיהּ בְּעוֹבָדֵי יְדָךְ כּוֹלָא שַׁוִיתָא תְחוֹת רִגְלוֹי:


You made him ruler over the works of your hands; all things you have placed under his feet.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 8:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/8:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/8:8)


עָאן וְתוֹרֵי כוּלְהוֹן וּלְחוֹד בְּעִירֵי חַקְלָא:


Sheep and oxen, all of them, and also the beasts of the field.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 8:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/8:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/8:9)


צִפְּרֵי שְׁמַיָא וְנוּנֵי יַמָא וְלִוְיָתָן דְחָלִיף אִסְרָטֵי יַמָא:


The birds of the air, and the fish of the sea, *and Leviathan* who passes along²⁶ the paths of the sea.

footnotes:
²⁶ burst from


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 8:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/8:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/8:10)


יְיָ אֱלָהָא רִבּוֹנָנָא כְּמָה תְלִיל וּמְשַׁבַּח שְׁמָךְ בְּכוּלָא אַרְעָא:


O God our master, how lofty *and praiseworthy* is your name in all the earth!



## Chapter 9

###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:1)


לְשַׁבָּחָא עַל מִיתוֹתָא דְגַבְרָא דִי נְפַק מִבֵּנֵי מַשִׁרְיָתָא תּוּשְׁבַּחְתָּא לְדָוִד:


For praise, concerning the death of the man²⁷ *who went out* between *the armies*.¹ A hymn of David.

footnotes:
²⁷ + the warrior
¹ For praise, concerning the sweetness of the sound by a son.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:2)


אֲשַׁבַּח קֳדָם יְיָ בְּכָל לִבִּי אֶשְׁתָּעֵי כּוּלְהוֹן פְּרִישְׁוָתָךְ:


I will sing praise *in the LORD's presence* with all my heart; I will tell all of your miracles.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:3)


אֶחְדֵי וְאֶבְדָח בְּמֵימְרָךְ אֲשַׁבַּח שְׁמָךְ עִלָאָה:


I will be glad and rejoice in *your word*; I will praise your name, O Most High.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:4)


בִּדְיְתוּב בְּעֵל דְבָבֵי לַאֲחוֹרָא יִתְקְלוּן וִיהוֹבְדוּן מִן קֳדָמָךְ:


When my enemies turn back, they will stumble and perish before you.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:5)


מְטוּל דַעֲבַדְתָּא פּוּרְעֲנוּתִי וְדִינִי יְתֵבְתָּא עַל כּוּרְסֵי דַיָנָא זַכָּאָה:


Because you have accomplished my vindication and my judgment; you sat down on the throne of the *righteous* judge.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:6)


נְזַפְתָּא עַמְמֵי דִפְלִשְׁתָּאֵי הוֹבַדְתָּא גָלְיַת רַשִׁיעָא שׁוּמְהוֹן מְחִיתָא לְעַלְמֵי עָלְמִין:


You rebuked the peoples *of the Philistines;* you destroyed *Goliath* the wicked; their name you erased forever and ever.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:7)


וְכַד נְפַל בְּעֵל דְבָבָא אִשְׁתֵּצָיוּ חֶלְוָתֵיהּ וּכְרַכֵּיהוֹן אִצְתַּדְיוּ לְעַלְמָא וְקִרְוֵיהוֹן צְדִיתָא הוֹבַדְתָּא דָכְרָנְהוֹן מִנְהוֹן:


*And when* the enemy *fell, his forces were obliterated,* and their fortresses were laid waste forever, and as for their cities, you destroyed the memory of them forever.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 9:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:8)


וּמֵימְרָא דַייָ לְעַלְמִין מוֹתְבֵיהּ בִּשְׁמֵי מְרוֹמָא תַקִין לְדִינָא כּוּרְסֵיהּ:


But as for *the word of* the LORD, his seat is in the highest heaven forever; he has established his throne for judgment.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 9:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:9)


וְהוּא יְדֵין עַמָא דְאַרְעָא בִּזְכוּתָא יְדִין עַמְמַיָא בִּתְרִיצוּתָא:


And he shall judge *the people of* the earth in righteousness; he will judge the Gentiles in uprightness.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 9:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:10)


וִיהֵי מֵימְרָא דַייָ תְּקוֹף לְמַסְכְּנָא תְּקוֹף בְּעִדָנֵי עַקְתָא:


And *the word of* the LORD will be strength to the poor, strength in times of distress.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 9:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:11)


וְיִסְתַּכְּלוּן בְּסַבָּרוּתָךְ יָדְעֵי שְׁמָךְ מְטוּל דְלָא שְׁבַקְתָּא תָּבְעָיִךְ יְיָ:


And those who know your name will look at²⁸ *your hope*²⁹, because you have not abandoned those who seek you, O LORD.

footnotes:
²⁸ trust in
²⁹ word


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 9:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:12)


שַׁבַּחוּ קֳדָם יְיָ דְאַשְׁרֵי שְׁכִנְתֵּיהּ בְּצִיוֹן תַּנִיאוּ בְּעַמְמַיָא עוֹבָדוֹי:


Sing praise before the Lord *who made his presence* rest in Zion; tell his deeds among the Gentiles.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 9:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:13)


מְטוּל דְתָבֵיעַ דְמָא דְזַכָּאָה יַת צַדִיקַיָא דְכִיר וְלָא שְׁלִי קְבֵלַת עִנְוְתָנִין:


For he avenges the *innocent* blood; he remembers *the righteous*, he does not neglect³⁰ the complaint of the humble.

footnotes:
³⁰ forget


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 9:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:14)


חוּס עֲלַי יְיָ חֲמֵי סִגוּפִי מִבַּעֲלֵי דְבָבִי מְרוֹמֵם לִי מִמַעֲלָנֵי מוֹתָא:


Pity me, O LORD; see my pain caused by my enemies³¹, you who lift me up from the entrances of death.

footnotes:
³¹ those who hate me


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 9:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:15)


מִן בִּגְלַל דְאִשְׁתָּעֵי לְכוּלְהוֹן שִׁבְחָתָךְ בְּמַעֲלָנֵי תַרְעֵי כְנִישַׁת צִיוֹן אַדוּץ בְּפוּרְקָנָךְ:


So that I may tell all your praises³² in the entrances of *the gates of the assembly of* Zion; I will exult in your redemption.

footnotes:
³² miracles


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 9:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:16)


טְבַעוּ עַמְמַיָא בְּשׁוּחֲתָא דַעֲבָדוּ בִּמְצַדְתָּא דְנָא כְּמַנוּ אִתְאֲחַדַת רִגְלֵיהוֹן:


The peoples have sunk in the pit that they made; in the very net they concealed, their feet are caught.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 9:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:17)


גְלֵי קֳדָם יְיָ דִין דַעֲבַד בְּעוֹבָדֵי אַיְדוֹי אִתְּקַל רַשִׁיעָא יְרַנְנוּן צַדִיקַיָא לְעָלְמִין:


Manifest before the LORD is the judgement he executed: through the works of his hands,³³ or he executed on the works of his hands.*the righteous will rejoice* forever.

footnotes:
³³ aaaaa


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 9:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:18)


יְתוּבוּן רַשִׁיעֵי לִשְׁיוֹל כּוּלְהוֹן עַמְמַיָא דְאַשְׁלִיוּ דְחַלְתָּא דַיְיָ:


The wicked will return to Sheol, all the Gentiles who neglected³⁴ *the fear of* the LORD.

footnotes:
³⁴ forgot


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 9:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:19)


מִטוּל דְלָא לְעָלְמַיָא מִשְׁתְּלֵי חֲשׁוֹכָא סוֹבָרָא דְעִנְוְתָנִי לָא תְהוֹבֵד לְעָלְמָא:


For the needy man is not forever neglected;³⁵ the hope of the humble will *not* perish forever.

footnotes:
³⁵ forgotten


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 9:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:20)


קוּם יְיָ לָא יִתְעַשַׁן בַּר נָשָׁא רַשִׁיעָא יִתְדָנוּן עַמְמַיָא קֳדָמָךְ:


Arise, O LORD, may the *wicked* son of man not grow strong, may the Gentiles be judged in your presence.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 9:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/9:21)


שַׁוִי יְיָ דְחַלְתָּא לְהוֹן יִנְדְעוּן עַמְמַיָא דְבַר נָשׁ הִינוּן לְעָלְמִין:


Put, O LORD, fear on them; let the peoples know that they are a son of man forever.



## Chapter 10

###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/10:1)


לָמָה יְיָ תְּקוּם בְּרָחִיק תִּטַמַר בִּמְדוֹר קַדִישִׁין לְעִדָנֵי עַקְתָא:


Why, O LORD, will you stand afar off, hide yourself *in the dwelling of the holy ones* in the times of distress?


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/10:2)


בְּגַסוֹת רַשִׁיעָא יִדְלַק עַנְיָא יִתְאַחְדוּן בְּנִכְלוּתָא דְנָא דִי חֲשַׁבוּ לְמֶעְבָּד:


In brutality³⁶ the wicked man will pursue the poor man; they will be caught in the scheme³⁷ that they plotted to carry out.

footnotes:
³⁶ pride
³⁷ folly


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/10:3)


מְטוּל דְאִשְׁתַּבַּח רַשִׁיעָא עַל רִגְרוּג נַפְשֵׁיהּ דִמְבָרַךְ גַבְרָא טָלוֹמָא מַרְחַק מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ:


For the wicked man is praised for the craving of his soul; he who blesses the violent man abhors *the word of* the LORD.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/10:4)


רַשִׁיעָא בְּגַסוּת רוּחֵיהּ לָא יִתְבַּר אֱלָהָא וְיֵימַר בְּלִבֵּיהּ דְלָא גַלְיָן קֳדָם יְיָ כּוּלְהוֹן מַחְשְׁבָתוֹי:


The wicked man in the grossness of his spirit will not seek *God*, and *he will say in his heart that his thoughts are not manifest in the presence of the LORD*.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/10:5)


מַצְלְחִין אוֹרְחָתֵיהּ בְּכָל עִדָן רְחִיקִין דִינָךְ מִן קִבְּלֵיהּ בְּכָל מְעִיקוֹי יִזְעוֹף בְּהוֹן:


His ways prosper at all times; your judgments are far from his sight; he will rebuke all his oppressors.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/10:6)


יֵימַר בִּלְבָבֵיהּ לָא אָזוּעַ מִדָר לְדָר מִלְמֶעֱבַד בִּישׁ:


He will say in his heart, “I will not be shaken from doing evil for all generations.”


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 10:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/10:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/10:7)


לוּטִין פּוּמֵיהּ מְלֵי נִכְלֵי וְרַמְיוֹתָא תְּחוֹת לִישָׁנֵיהּ לֵאוֹת וּשְׁקָר:


His mouth is curses, full of guile and deceit; under his tongue is misery and falsehood.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 10:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/10:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/10:8)


יָתֵיב בִּכְמַנֵי דְדַרְתַיָא בְּטוּמָרַיָא יִקְטוֹל זַכָּאָה עֵינוֹי לְמַסְכְּנַיָא יִטְשׁוּן:


He will sit in the hiding places of the courtyards; in secret places he will kill the innocent; he will hide his eyes against the poor.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 10:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/10:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/10:9)


יִכְמוֹן בְּטוּמָרַיָא הֵיךְ אַרְיָא בְּמַטְלָלֵיהּ יִכְמוֹן לְמִתְפַּשׂ עַנְיָא יִתְפַּשׂ עַנְיָא בְּמִגָּרֵיהּ בִּמְצַדְתֵּיהּ:


He will lie in wait in secret places like a lion in his covert; he will lie in wait to seize the poor man; he will seize the poor man when he is drawn into his trap.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 10:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/10:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/10:10)


יְדַכְדַךְ וִישׁוּחַ וְיִפֹּל בִּתְקוֹף בְּמָנוֹי עַנְיָא:


The poor man will be crushed, and sink down, and he will fall into the power of his hiding places.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 10:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/10:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/10:11)


יֵימַר בְּלִבֵּיהּ אִתְנְשֵׁי אֱלָהָא טְמַר אַפּוֹי לָא חָמֵי לְעָלְמִין:


He will say in his heart, “God has forgotten, he has hidden his face, he does not see forever.”


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 10:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/10:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/10:12)


קוּם יְיָ קַיָם שְׁבוּעַת יְדָךְ לָא תִנְשִׁי עִנְוְתָנֵי:


Arise, O LORD³⁸, fulfill the oath of your hand, do not forget the humble.

footnotes:
³⁸ + our God


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 10:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/10:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/10:13)


מְטוּל מָה רָחֵיק רַשִׁיעָא אֱלָהָא יֵימַר בְּלִבֵּיהּ לָא תִתְבְּעֵי:


Why has the wicked man abhorred God? He will say³⁹ in his heart, “It will not be sought after.”⁴⁰

footnotes:
³⁹ Tg יימר suggests Heb יאמר.
⁴⁰ "You will not seek."


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 10:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/10:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/10:14)


גְלֵי קֳדָמָךְ מְטוּל דְאַנְתְּ לֵאוֹת וּרְגִיז עַל רַשִׁיעָא תַּסְעֵר אִסְתַּכַּל לְמִשְׁלַם אַגְרָא טָב לְצַדִיקַיָא בִּידָךְ עֲלָךְ יְסוֹבְרוּן עַנְיָךְ יַתֵּם אַנְתְּ הֲוֵיתָ סָעֵיד:


It is manifest in your presence, because you *will inflict* misery and wrath *upon the wicked man*; look carefully to pay *a good reward to the righteous* by your hand; the poor will place *their hope* on you; you have been a helper to the orphan.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 10:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/10:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/10:15)


תְּבַר אֶדְרָעֵיהוֹן דְרַשִׁיעֵי וּבִישֵׁי יִתְבְּעוּן רִשְׁעַתְהוֹן לָא יִשְׁכְּחוּ:


Break the arm of the wicked; and let the evil seek their wickedness, [and] not find it.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 10:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/10:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/10:16)


יְיָ מְלִיךְ לְעָלְמֵי עָלְמַיָא אֲבַדוּ עַמְמֵי מֵאַרְעֵיהּ:


The LORD is king forever and ever; the Gentiles have perished⁴¹ from his land.

footnotes:
⁴¹ will perish


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 10:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/10:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/10:17)


רִיגְתָּא דְעִנְוְתָנֵי שְׁמִיעַ קֳדָמָךְ יְיָ תַּתְקֵין לִבְּהוֹן תַּצְלֵי אוּדְנָךְ:


The desire of the humble is heard *in your presence*, O LORD; strengthen their heart, incline your ear⁴².

footnotes:
⁴² + to their prayer


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 10:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/10:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/10:18)


לְמִדַן יַתֵּם וְעַנְיָא לָא יוֹסִיף עוֹד תּוּב בְּנֵי נְשָׁא לְאִתְוָרָא מִן קֳדָם רַשִׁיעֵי אַרְעָא:


To judge the orphan and poor man; may the sons of men not again be shattered *before the wicked of the earth.*



## Chapter 11

###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/11:1)


תּוּשְׁבַּחְתָּא לְדָוִד בְּמֵימְרָא דַייָ סַבְּרֵית הֵכְדֵין אַתּוּן אָמְרִין לְנַפְשִׁי אִטַלְטְלֵי לְטוּרָא הֵיךְ צִפָּרָא:


A hymn of David. In *the word of* the LORD I have hoped; how do you say to my soul, wander to the mountain⁴³ like a bird?

footnotes:
⁴³ your mountain


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/11:2)


אֲרוּם הָא רַשִׁיעֵי נַגְדִין קַשְׁתָּא מְתַקְנִין גִירֵהוֹן עַל נִימָא לְמִרְמֵי בִּקְבָלָא עֲלַוֵי תַקִינֵי לִבָּא:


For behold, the wicked bend the bow, fixing their arrows on the string to shoot in darkness at the firm⁴⁴ of heart.

footnotes:
⁴⁴ upright


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/11:3)


מְטוּל דְאִין אִשְׁיָתָא יִתְרַעוּן זַכָּאָה מְטוּלָא עֲבַד טוּבָא:


For *if* the foundations are shattered, why did the virtuous do good?⁴⁵

footnotes:
⁴⁵ righteous do right


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/11:4)


יְיָ בְּהֵיכְלָא דְקוּדְשֵׁיהּ אֱלָהָא בִשְׁמֵי מְרוֹמָא כּוּרְסֵיהּ עֵינוֹי חָמְיָן תְּמוֹרוֹי מְבַחְנָן בְּנֵי נָשָׁא:


The LORD is in his holy temple; God’s⁴⁶ throne is in the *highest* heavens; his eyes see, his eyelids examine, the sons of men.

footnotes:
⁴⁶ the LORD's


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/11:5)


אֱלָהָא צַדִיקַיָא בָחֵין וְרַשִׁיעַיָא וְרַחֲמֵי חֲטוּפִין סְנָאַת נַפְשֵׁיהּ:


God⁴⁷ examines the righteous, but his soul hates the wicked and those who love rapacity.

footnotes:
⁴⁷ the word of the LORD


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 11:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/11:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/11:6)


יָחֵית מַטְרִין דְפוּרְעָנוּ עַל רַשִׁיעֵי מַפְּחִין דְאֶשְׁתָּא וְכַבְרִיתָא וְזַעֲפָא עַלְעוֹלָא יַהֲבַת כַּלִידְהוֹן:


He will bring down rains *of retribution* on the wicked, coals of fire and brimstone; a violent storm-wind is the portion of their cup.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 11:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/11:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/11:7)


מְטוּל דְצַדִיקָא יְיָ צִדְקוּתָא רָחֵים תְּקַנְיָא יֶחֱמוֹן סְבַר אַפּוֹי:


For the LORD is righteous, he loves righteousness,⁴⁸ the honest man will look upon *his* countenance.

footnotes:
⁴⁸ virtue



## Chapter 12

###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/12:1)


לְשַׁבָחָא עַל כִּנָרָא דְתַמְנַיָא נִימֵי תּוּשְׁבַּחְתָּא לְדָוִד:


For praise, on *the lyre of* eight *strings*. A hymn of David.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/12:2)


פְּרוֹק יְיָ מְטוּל דִגְמִירוּ טָבַיָא אֲרוּם סָפוּ מְהֵימְנַיָא מִן בְּנֵי נָשָׁא:


Redeem, O LORD, for the good⁴⁹ are annihilated; for the faithful have ceased from the sons of men.

footnotes:
⁴⁹ righteous


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/12:3)


שִׁקְרָא מְמַלְלִין אֱנָשׁ עִם חַבְרֵיהּ סִפְוָתָא שְׁעִיאָן בִּלְבָבֵהוֹן מְנַבְּלִין וּבְלִבָּא שִׁקְרָא מְמַלְלִין:


They speak lies, each to his fellow, lips are flattering; in *their* heart *they deceive,* and with *a lying* heart they speak.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/12:4)


יְשֵׁיצֵי יְיָ מִן עַלְמָא כָּל סִפְוָן שְׁעִיעִין לִישָׁנָא דְמַלְלָא רַבְרְבָנוּתָא:


The LORD will destroy *from the world* all flattering lips, the tongue that speaks arrogance.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 12:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/12:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/12:5)


מִן דְכָפְרִין בְּעִקְרָא דְאָמְרִין בְּלִישָׁנָנָא נִתְגַבַּר סִפְוָתָנָא עִמָנָא מַן רִבּוֹן דִלָנָא:


Those *who deny the essence,* who say, “By our tongue we shall prevail, our lips are with us, who is our master?”


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 12:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/12:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/12:6)


מֵאוּנְסָא דַעֲנִיֵי מִן צְוָחַת חֲשִׁיכֵי הַשְׁתָּא אֲקוּם אֲמַר יְיָ אֲשַׁוֵי פוּרְקַן וּלְרַשִׁיעֵי אַסְחֵיר בִּישׁוּתָא לְהוֹן:


Because of the oppression of the poor, because of the cry of the needy, now I will arise,⁵⁰ says the LORD; I will give redemption *to my people, but against the wicked* I will give testimony *of evil.*

footnotes:
⁵⁰ + in judgement


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 12:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/12:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/12:7)


מִלִין דַייָ מִלַיָא דַכְיָן סִימָא סְנִינָא בְּכוּרָא עַל אַרְעָא זְקִיק שִׁבְעָתֵי זִמְנִין:


The words of the Lord are pure words, silver⁵¹ purified in the furnace on the ground, refined seven times.

footnotes:
⁵¹ like silver


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 12:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/12:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/12:8)


אַנְתְּ יְיָ תִּנְטְרִנוּן לְצַדִיקַיָא תִּנְצְרִנוּן מִן דָרָא בִישָׁא הָדֵין לְעַלְמִין:


You, O Lord, will keep *the righteous*; you will protect *them*⁵² from this evil generation forever.

footnotes:
⁵² Tg תנטרינון suggests Heb תשמרם


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 12:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/12:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/12:9)


חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר רַשִׁיעַיָא מְהַלְכִין כַּעֲלוּקָא דְמָצְצָא דְמֵיהוֹן דִבְנֵי נָשָׁא:


All around the wicked walk, like a leech *that sucks the blood of* the sons of men.



## Chapter 13

###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/13:1)


לְשַׁבָּחָא תּוּשְׁבַּחְתָּא לְדָוִד:


For praise, a hymn of David.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/13:2)


עַד אָן יְיָ תַּשְׁלִינַנִי לְעָלְמִין עַד אָן תִּטְמוֹר זִיו אַפָּךְ מִנִי:


How long, O LORD, will you neglect me forever? How long will you hide *the splendor of* your face from me?


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/13:3)


עַד אָן אֲשַׁוֵי מִלְכָּא בְּנַפְשִׁי דְוָנָא בִּלְבָבִי יְמָם עַד אָן יִתְּרָם בְּעֵל דְבָבִי עֲלָי:


How long will I put warnings in my soul, suffering in my heart daily? How long will my enemy vaunt himself over me?


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 13:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/13:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/13:4)


אִסְתַּכֵּל וְקַבֵּל צְלוֹתִי יְיָ אֱלָהִי אַנְהִיר עֵינַי בְּאוֹרַיַתָךְ דִלְמָא אֱחוֹב וְאֶדְמוּךְ עִם חַיָבֵי מוֹתָא:


Pay heed and *receive my prayer*, O LORD my God; illumine my eyes *by your Torah*, lest *I sin and* sleep *with those who deserve* death.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 13:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/13:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/13:5)


דִלְמָא יֵימַר יִצְרָא בִישָׁא אַשְׁלַטְתֵּיהּ מְעִיקַי יְרַנֵנוּן מְטוּל אֶסְטֵי מֵאָרְחָתָךְ:


Lest the *evil impulse*⁵³ should say, “I have taken control of him,” [lest] my oppressors rejoice because I stray⁵⁴ *from your paths.*

footnotes:
⁵³ my enemy
⁵⁴ have strayed


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 13:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/13:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/13:6)


וַאֲנָא בְּטוּבָךְ אִתְרַחֲצֵית יְרַנֵן לְבָבִי בְּפוּרְקָנָךְ אֲשַׁבַּח קֳדָם יְיָ מְטוּל דִפְרִיעַ עֲלָי טָבְתָא:


But I have placed my trust in your goodness, my heart will rejoice in your redemption; I will give praise in the Lord’s presence because he rewards me *with good things.*



## Chapter 14

###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/14:1)


לְשַׁבָּחָא בְּרוּחַ נְבוּאָה עַל דָוִד אֲמַר שַׁטְיָא בִּלְבָבֵיהּ לֵית שׁוּלְטָנָא דֶאֱלָהָא בְאַרְעָא חֲבִילוּ עוֹבָדֵיהוֹן רָחֲקוּ טָבְתָא וְאַשְׁכָּחוּ עִלָא לֵית דַעֲבַד טָב:


For praise; *in the spirit of prophecy through* David. The fool⁵⁵ said in his heart, “There is no *rule of* God on the earth.” They corrupted *their deeds*, they despised *goodness and found* iniquity. There is none who does good.

footnotes:
⁵⁵ stupid man


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/14:2)


יְיָ מִשְׁמַיָא אוֹדִיק עַל בְּנֵי נָשָׁא לְמֶחֱמֵי אִין אִית מַשְׂכִּיל תָּבַע אוּלְפַן מִן קֳדָם יְיָ:


The LORD looked down from heaven on the sons of men⁵⁶ to see if there was any wise man seeking *instruction from the presence of* the LORD.

footnotes:
⁵⁶ Adam


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/14:3)


כּוּלְהוֹן זָרוּ לַאֲחוֹרָא כַּחֲדָא אִתְּרַשֵׁלוּ לֵית דְעָבַד טָב אֲפִילוּ לֵית חָד:


All alike have turned backward, they have become lax; there is none who does good, there is not even one.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 14:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/14:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/14:4)


הֲלָא יְדָעוּ כָּל עַבְדֵי שְׁקַר סָעֲדֵי עַמִי סָעֲדוּ לַחְמָא שְׁמָא דַייָ לָא בָרִיכוּ:


Do they not know, all doers of falsehood? Those among my people who dine have dined on bread [and] not *blessed the name of* the LORD.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 14:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/14:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/14:5)


תַמָן דַחֲלוּ דַחֲלָא מְטוּל דְמֵימְרָא דַייָ בְּדָרָא דְצַדִיקֵי:


There they became afraid⁵⁷ because *the word of* the LORD is in the generation of the righteous.

footnotes:
⁵⁷ they worshipped a false gold that is not proper to worship.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 14:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/14:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/14:6)


מִלְכַּת עַנְיָא תְּבַהֲתוּן מְטוּל דְשַׁוֵי בַיָי סִבְרֵיהּ:


You will despise the counsel of the poor man,⁵⁸ because he has placed his hope in the LORD.

footnotes:
⁵⁸ + who are humble, who rebuke them


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 14:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/14:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/14:7)


מַן יַרְחִישׁ מִצִיוֹן פּוּרְקָנָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל כַּד יְתִיב יְיָ גְלוּת עַמֵיהּ יְרַנֵן יַעֲקֹב יֶחֱדֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


Who will produce from Zion the redemption of Israel? When the LORD brings back the exile of his people, Jacob will rejoice, Israel will be glad.



## Chapter 15

###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/15:1)


תּוּשְׁבַּחְתָּא לְדָוִד יְיָ מַן חֲמֵי לְמֵידַר בְּמִשְׁכָּנָךְ מַן חֲמֵי לְמִשְׁרֵי בְּטוּר בֵית מַקְדְשָׁךְ:


A hymn of David. O LORD, who *is worthy* to dwell in your tabernacle, who *is worthy*⁵⁹ to abide on the mountain of your sanctuary?

footnotes:
⁵⁹ deserves


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/15:2)


דִמְהֲלִיךְ בִשְׁלִימָתָא וְעָבֵד צִדְקָתָא וּמְמַלֵל קוּשְׁטָא בִּלְבָבֵיהּ:


One who walks in integrity, and does righteous deeds, and speaks truth in his heart.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/15:3)


לָא אֲכַל קוּרְצִין בְּלִישְׁנֵיהּ לָא עֲבַד לְחַבְרֵיהּ בִּישׁוּתָא וְכִסוֹפָא לָא סוֹבֵר עַל קְרִיבֵיהּ:


He does not slander with his tongue, he causes no harm to his fellow, and he bears no shame⁶⁰ against his neighbor.

footnotes:
⁶⁰ disgrace


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/15:4)


דְשָׁט בְּאַנְפּוֹי וּבְסִיר וְיַת דַחֲלַיָא דַייָ מוֹקִיר דְיוֹמֵי לְאַבְאָשָׁא לְגַרְמֵיהּ וְלָא מְשַׁלְחָף:


Who despises the contemptible to his face, but honors those who fear the LORD; who will swear to do harm *to himself* and does not change.⁶¹

footnotes:
⁶¹ reverse


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 15:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/15:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/15:5)


סִימֵיהּ לָא יְהַב בְּחִבּוּלְיָא וְשׁוֹחֲדָא עַל זַכָּאָה לָא קַבֵל דַעֲבֵיד אִלֵין לָא יְזוּעַ לְעַלְמָא:


He has not given his money at interest; he has not accepted a bribe against the innocent; one who does these things will never be moved.⁶²

footnotes:
⁶² shaken



## Chapter 16

###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/16:1)


גְלִיפָא תְרִיצָא לְדָוִד נְטַר יָתִי אֱלָהָא מְטוּל דְסַבְרֵית בְּמֵימְרָךְ:


An honest inscription of David. Protect me, O God, because I have hoped⁶³ in *your word.*

footnotes:
⁶³ trusted


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/16:2)


מַלֵילְתְּ אַנְתְּ נַפְשִׁי קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהִי אַנְתְּ בְּרַם טִיבָתִי לָא מִתְיְהִיבָא בַּר מִנָךְ:


You have spoken – *you, my soul – in the presence of* the LORD. You are my God, *truly* my goodness is not *present* without you.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/16:3)


לְקַדִישַׁיָא דִי בְאַרְעָא הִנוּן הוֹדַעוּ כֹּחַ גְבוּרְתִּי מִן שְׁרוּיָא וְגֵוְתָנִין בְּעוֹבָדֵיהוֹן טָבַיָא כָּל רְעוּתִי טָב בְּהוּ:


To the holy ones that are in the in the land *they have declared the might of my power from the beginning;* and as for those proud *of their good deeds,* my *good* will is for them.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/16:4)


מַסְגָן צַלְמָנֵיהוֹן מִן בָּתַר כֵּן מוֹחָן לְקָרָבָא קוּרְבָּנֵיהוֹן לָא אֲקַבֵּל בְּרַעֲוָא נְסוּכֵהוֹן וְדַם קוּרְבָּנֵיהוֹן וְלָא אֶדְכַּר שׁוּמְהוֹן עַל סִפְוָתִי:


But the wicked multiply their idols; afterwards they hurry *to make their sacrifices.* I will not receive *favorably* their libations *or* the blood *of their sacrifices,* nor will I mention their name with my lips.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 16:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/16:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/16:5)


יְיָ מוֹהָבִית כַּלִידִי וְחוּלָקִי אַנְתְּ תְּסוֹבֵר עַדְבִי:


The LORD is the portion of my cup and my share; you will support⁶⁴ my lot.

footnotes:
⁶⁴ take care of.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 16:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/16:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/16:6)


עַדְבִין נְפַלוּ לִי בִּבְסִימָתָא אוּף אַחֲסַנְתָּא שַׁפִּירָא עֲלָי:


The lots have fallen pleasantly for me; indeed, a beautiful inheritance is mine.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 16:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/16:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/16:7)


אֲבָרֵךְ יַת יְיָ דְמַלְכִּינָנִי אוּף בְּלֵילְיָא דְדָאָן לִי כוּלְיָתָי:


I will bless the LORD, who has counseled me; even at night my mind⁶⁵ disciplines me.

footnotes:
⁶⁵ lit: kidneys.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 16:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/16:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/16:8)


שִׁוִיתִי יְיָ לְקוּבְּלִי תְּדִירָא מְטוּל דִשְׁכִנְתֵּיהּ שַׁרְיָא עֲלַי לָא אַזוּעַ:


I have placed the LORD⁶⁶ before me always, for h*is presence rests on me;*⁶⁷ I shall not be shaken.

footnotes:
⁶⁶ the word of the LORD.
⁶⁷ at my right hand.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 16:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/16:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/16:9)


בְּגִין חֲדָא לִבִּי וְרַנֵן אַיְקָרִי אוֹף בִּשְׂרֵי יִשְׁרֵי לְרוֹחֲצָן:


Therefore my heart is glad, and my glory rejoices; besides, my flesh shall dwell in security.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 16:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/16:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/16:10)


מְטוּל דְלָא תִשְׁבּוֹק נַפְשִׁי לִשְׁיוֹל לָא תִמְסוֹר זַכָּאָךְ לְמֶחֱמֵי בְּשִׁחֲיוּתָא:


For you will not abandon my soul to Sheol, you will not hand over your innocent one to see corruption.⁶⁸

footnotes:
⁶⁸ place of corruption.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 16:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/16:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/16:11)


תְּהוֹדַע לִי אוֹרְחָא דְחַיֵי סוֹבְעָא דְחֶדְוָתָא קֳדָם אַפָּךְ בְּסִימָתָא בִּימִינָךְ לְעַלְמִין:


You will tell me the way of life; abundance of joy is in the presence of your face; pleasant things are at your right hand forever.



## Chapter 17

###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/17:1)


צְלוֹתָא לְדָוִד קַבֵּל יְיָ פְּגִיעֲתִי בִּצְדָקָה אֲצִית שַׁבּוּחִי תַּצְלֵי אוּדְנָךְ לִצְלוֹתִי בִּדְלָא סְפְוָתֵי נִכְלָא:


A prayer of David. Accept, O LORD, my entreaty;⁶⁹ in righteousness hear my praise; you will incline your ear to my prayer, since my lips are without guile.

footnotes:
⁶⁹ request


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/17:2)


מִן קֳדָמָךְ דִינִי יִפּוֹק עֵינָךְ תֶּהֶוְיָן חָמִין תְּרִיצוּתָא:


From your presence my judgment shall come forth; your eyes will behold honesty.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/17:3)


בְּחַנְתָּא לִבְבִי אַסְעַרְתָּא עֲלָי בְּלֵילְיָא סְנַנְתָּא לִי לָא אַשְׁכַּחְתָּא שְׁחִיתָא חַשְׁבֵית בִּישׁוּתָא לָא עֲבַר פּוּמִי:


You have tested my heart; you have visited me at night; you have purified⁷⁰ me [and] not found corruption. [If] I thought of evil, it has not passed my mouth.

footnotes:
⁷⁰ tested


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 17:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/17:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/17:4)


בְּרַם אַכְסַנְתָּא עַל עוֹבָדֵיהוֹן דִבְנֵי נָשָׁא בְּמֵימַר סִפְוָתָךְ אֲנָא נְטַרֵית אָרְחוֹת חֲצִיפָא:


*Truly you have rebuked* the deeds of the sons of men by the word of your lips; I have kept [myself from] the ways of audacity.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 17:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/17:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/17:5)


סְעֵד אִסְתַּוְרַי בַּהֲלִיכָתָךְ דְלָא יְזוּעוּן רִגְלָי:


Support my steps in your path, lest my feet be shaken.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 17:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/17:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/17:6)


אֲנָא קְרֵיתִי יָתָךְ מְטוּל תְּקַבֵּל צְלוֹתִי אֱלָהָא צַלֵי אוּדְנָךְ קַבֵּל צְלוֹתִי:


I have called you because you will receive my prayer, O God; incline your ear, receive my prayer⁷¹.

footnotes:
⁷¹ hear my speech


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 17:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/17:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/17:7)


פָּרֵישׁ טַבְוָתָךְ פָּרִיקְהוֹן דְסָבְרֵי מִן מַן דְקַיְמִין עֲלֵיהוֹן בְּאַיְדָא דִימִינָךְ:


Display your goodness,⁷² O redeemer of those who hope;⁷³ from those who rise up *against them* by your right hand.

footnotes:
⁷² merits.
⁷³ + and avenge them


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 17:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/17:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/17:8)


נְטַר יָתִי כְּגִלְגוּל דִי בִמְצִיעוּת עֵינָא בְטוּלָא דִשְׁכִנְתָּךְ תִּטְמוֹר יָתִי:


Guard me like the circle⁷⁴ that is in *the middle of* the eye; in the shadow of your *presence* you will hide me.

footnotes:
⁷⁴ the pupil of the circle


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 17:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/17:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/17:9)


מִן קֳדָם רַשִׁיעַיָא אִלֵין דִמְחַבְּלִין יָתִי בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבִי בִּרְעוּת נַפְשֵׁיהוֹן מְחַזְרָן עֲלָי:


From the presence of the wicked, those who harm me; my enemies, in the desire of their soul, surround me.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 17:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/17:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/17:10)


עָתְרְהוֹן סְגָא שׁוֹמְנֵיהוֹן כַּסִיאוּ פּוּמְהוֹן מַלְלוּ בְּרַבְרְבוּתָא:


*Their wealth has increased,* their fat covers [them], their mouth has spoken arrogantly.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 17:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/17:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/17:11)


אִסְתַּוְרָנָא הַשְׁתָּא חֲזַרוּ לָנָא עֵינֵיהוֹן מְשַׁוָן לְמִפְשַׁט בְּאַרְעָא:


Our steps now have surrounded us; their eyes are fixed to extend throughout the land.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 17:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/17:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/17:12)


דְמוּתֵיהּ הֵיךְ אַרְיָא דִמְרַגֵג לְמִתְבַּר וּכְשַׁחֲלָא דְיָתִיב בְּטוּמְרַיָא:


He resembles a lion who yearns to tear, or a jungle-cat that dwells in secret places.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 17:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/17:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/17:13)


קוּם יְיָ אַקְדֵם אַפּוֹי חַמְטֵיהּ פְּצָא נַפְשִׁי מִן רַשִׁיעָא דְאִתְחַיֵיב קְטוֹל בְּסַיְפָךְ:


Arise, O LORD⁷⁵, forestall him, strike him down; deliver my soul from the wicked man *who deserves death* by your sword.

footnotes:
⁷⁵ God


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 17:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/17:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/17:14)


וְצַדִיקַיָא דְמַסְרִין נַפְשֵׁיהוֹן מְטוּלְתָּךְ יְיָ לְמִיתוּתָא בְּאַרְעָא חוּלַקְהוֹן בְּחַיֵי עָלְמָא וְטַשְׁיוּתָךְ טָבָא יִתְמַלְיָן כְּרֵיסֵיהוֹן יִסְבְּעוּן בְּנִין וְיִשְׁבְּקוּן שִׁיוּרְהוֹן לְטַלְיֵהוֹן:


*And the righteous who hand over their souls on your account,* O LORD, *to death⁷⁶ in the land*, their portion is in *eternal* life, and their bellies will be filled with your *good* store; children will be satisfied, and they will leave their surplus⁷⁷ to their children.

footnotes:
⁷⁶ + and are destroyed
⁷⁷ profit


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 17:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/17:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/17:15)


אֲנָא בְּקוּשְׁטָא אֶחְמֵי סְבַר אַפָּךְ אֶשְׂבַּע בְּעֵדֶן דְאִתְעָר מִן אַיְקָר פַּרְצוּפָךְ:


I in truth will see your countenance, I will be satisfied *at the time that/ifrom the glory of* your face.



## Chapter 18

###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:1)


לְשַׁבָּחָא עַל נִסַיָא דְאִתְרְחִישׁוּ לְעַבְדָא דַייָ לְדָוִד דְשַׁבָּח בִּנְבוּאָה קֳדָם יְיָ יַת פִּתְגָמֵי שִׁירְתָא הָדָא עַל כָּל יוֹמַיָא דְשֵׁזְבֵיהּ יְיָ מִן יְדָא דְכָל בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבוֹי וּמֵחַרְבָּא דְשָׁאוּל:


For praise. About *the miracles that occurred to* the servant of the LORD, David, who sang *in prophecy* in the presence of the LORD the words of this song about all the days that the LORD delivered him from the hand of all his enemies and from *the sword of* Saul.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:2)


וַאֲמַר אֲחַבְּבִנָךְ יְיָ תּוּקְפִי:


And he said: I will love you, O LORD, my strength.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:3)


יְיָ תּוּקְפִי וְרֻחְצָנִי וּמְשֵׁזִיב יָתִי אֱלָהָא דְאִתְרְעֵי בִּי קָרְבַנִי לִדְחַלְתֵּיהּ תְּרֵיסִי דְמִן קֳדָמוֹי אִתְיְהַב לִי תְקוֹף וּפוּרְקָן עַל בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבֵי רָחְצָנִי:


O LORD, my strength and my security and the one who delivers me; *the God who has chosen me has brought me near to fear him;⁷⁸ my shield, from whose presence is given me strength and* redemption *over my enemies;* my security.

footnotes:
⁷⁸ to his fear


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 18:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:4)


אֲמַר דָוִד בְתוּשְׁבַּחְתָּא אֲנָא מְצַלֵי קֳדָם יְיָ וּמִן בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבִי פָּרִיק יָתִי:


*David said* in praise: “I pray in the LORD’s presence, and from my enemies he redeems me.”


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 18:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:5)


אַקְפַתְנִי עָקָא כְאִתָּא דְיָתְבָא עַל מַתְבְּרָא וְחֵיל לֵית לָהּ לְמֵילַד וְהִיא מְסַכְּנָא לְמֵמָת וְסִיעַת טָלוֹמַיָא בַּעֲתַת יָתִי:


*Distress* has surrounded me, *like a woman who sits on the birthstool and has no strength to give birth and so is in danger of* death; *a band of* abusive men has terrified me.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 18:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:6)


מַשִׁרְיַת חַיָבִין אַקְפוּנִי אַקְדוּמִי יָתִי דִמְזַיְנִין בְּמָנֵי קְטוֹל:


*Armies of sinners* have surrounded me; *those armed with deadly weapons* have confronted me.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 18:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:7)


בִּדְעָיְקָא לִי אֲנָא מְצַלֵי קֳדָם יְיָ וּקְדָם אֱלָהִי אֲנָא מִתְחַנֵן וּמְקַבֵּל צְלוֹתִי מִן הֵיכְלֵיהּ וּבָעוּתִי קֳדָמוֹי מִתְקַבְּלָא בְּאוּדְנוֹי וּמִתְעַבְדָא:


When I am in distress, I pray in the presence of the LORD; and in the presence of my God I make supplication; and he accepts my *prayer* from his temple,⁷⁹ and my petition in his presence is received by his ears, *and is granted.*

footnotes:
⁷⁹ from his holy temple in heaven


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 18:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:8)


וְאִתְרְגִיפַת וְאִתְרְגִישַׁת אַרְעָא וְאֻשְׁיָת טוּרַיָא זְעוּ וְאִתְרְטִישׁוּ מְטוּל דִתְקֵיף לֵיהּ:


The earth trembled and shook and the foundations of the mountains tottered, and split, for he was angry with it.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 18:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:9)


סְלִיק זְדוֹנֵיהּ דְפַרְעֹה הֵיךְ קוּטְרָא דִבְכֵן שְׁלַח רוּגְזֵיהּ כְּאֶשָׁא בָּעֲרָא דְמִן קֳדָמוֹי מִשְׁתֵּצְיָא מְזוֹפִיתֵיהּ כְּגוּמְרִין דִי נוּר דָלְקָא מִמֵימְרֵיהּ:


*The arrogance of Pharaoh*⁸⁰ went up like smoke;⁸¹ *then he sent his anger* like a *burning* fire that consumes⁸² before him; his *rebuke* burns *at his utterance* like coals *of fire.*

footnotes:
⁸⁰ + the wicked
⁸¹ + before him
⁸² reading מישיצייא

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:10)


וְאַרְכֵן שְׁמַיָא וְאִתְגְלֵי יְקָרֵיהּ וַעֲנַן אֲמִטְתָא כַּבֵּשׁ קֳדָמוֹי:


And he bent down the heavens, and *his glory was manifested,* a *dark* cloud *a path before* him.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 18:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:11)


וְאִתְגְלֵי בִּגְבוּרְתֵּיהּ עַל כְּרוּבִין קַלִילִין וּדְבַר בִּתְקוֹף עַל גַדְפֵי זַעֲפָא:


So he *was manifested in his strength* over *swift* cherubs; and he *proceeded in might* on the wings of the storm-wind.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 18:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:12)


וְאַשְׁרֵי שְׁכִינְתֵּיהּ בְּעַרְפְּלָא וְאִתְחְזַר בַּעֲנָנֵי יְקָרֵיהּ הֵיךְ מְטַלַלְתָּא וְאַחֵית מִטְרִין דְרַעֲוָא עַל עַמֵיהּ וּמַיִן תַּקִיפִין מִן רִכְפַּת עֲנָנִין דְקִבְּלָא עַל רַשִׁיעַיָא מִן רוּמֵיהּ דְעָלְמָא:


And he made *his presence dwell* in the mist, and surrounded *himself with the clouds of his glory* as a covering; *and he made favorable rains to fall on his people, and mighty* waters *from the massed clouds of* darkness *on the wicked from the eternal* heights.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:13)


מִן זְהוֹר יְקָרֵיהּ עֲנָנֵי שְׁמַיָא עֲבַרוּ בִּמְזוֹפִיתָא בְּגוּמְרִין דְנוּר בַּרְדָא דָלְקָא מִן מֵימְרֵיהּ:


From the splendor *of his glory* the clouds *of heaven* passed by *in rebuke like* the coals of fire and *burning* hail *from his word.*

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:14)


וְאַכְלֵי מִן שְׁמַיָא יְיָ וְעִלָאָה אֲרִים מֵימְרֵיהּ רָמָא בַרְדָא וְגוּמְרִין דִי נוּר:


And the LORD *gave a shout* from heaven, and the Most High raised up his utterance; *he cast* hail and coals of fire.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:15)


וְשַׁדַר מֵימְרֵיהּ הֵיךְ גִירִין וּבַרְדִין וּבַרְקִין סַגִיאִין יְשַׂגְשְׂגוּן:


And he sent *his word like* arrows, and scattered them; [he sent] many lightning bolts, and confounded them.
