[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:16)


וְאִתְחַמִיאוּ עִמְקֵי מַיָא וְאִתְגְלִיאוּ שִׁתְאֲסַיָא דְתֵבֵל מִמְזוֹפִיתָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ מִמֵימַר תְּקוֹף רוּגְזָךְ:


And the depths of the *sea*⁸³ became visible, and the pillars of the world were uncovered at the rebuke of the LORD, from the *utterance* of your mighty wrath.

⁸³ following 2 Sam. 22:16.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 18:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:17)


שְׁלַח נְבִיוֹהִי מֶלֶךְ תַּקִיף דְיָתֵיב בִּתְקוֹף רוּמָא דַבְּרַנִי שֵׁזְבַנִי מֵעַמְמִין סַגִיאִין:


He sent *his prophets, [he who is] a mighty king who reigns in strength;*⁸⁴ he took me [and] delivered me from many *Gentiles.*

footnotes:
⁸⁴ + on high


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 18:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:18)


שֵׁזְבַנִי מִן סָנְאַי אֲרוּם עֲשִׁינִין הִינוּן וּמִן בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבִי אֲרוּם אִתְגַבַּרוּ עֲלָי:


He delivered me from my enemies, *for they are* strong; from my foes, for they prevailed *against* me.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 18:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:19)


אַקְדִימוּ לִי בְיוֹם טִלְטוּלִי וַהֲוָה מֵימְרָא דַייָ סָמִיךְ לִי:


They confronted me in the day of my *wandering;* but *the word of* the LORD was my support.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 18:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:20)


וְאַפֵּק יָתִי לִרְוָחָא שֵׁזְבַנִי מְטוּל דְאִתְרְעֵי בִי:


And he brought me out to a broad place, he delivered me because he was pleased with me.⁸⁵

⁸⁵ + said David


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 18:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:21)


יִגְמְלִנַנִי יְיָ כִזְכוּתִי כִּבְרִירוּת יְדֵי יְתִיב לִי:


The LORD will requite me⁸⁶ according to my merit; according to the cleanness of my hands he will answer me.

footnotes:
⁸⁶ repay me


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 18:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:22)


מְטוּל דִנְטָרֵית אוֹרְחָן דְתַקְנָן קֳדָם יְיָ וְלָא הַלְכֵית בִּרְשַׁע קֳדָם יְיָ:


For I have kept the *proper* ways in the LORD’s presence; and I have not *walked in* evil before the *LORD*.⁸⁷

footnotes:
⁸⁷ my God


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 18:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:23)


מְטוּל דְכָל דִינוֹ גְלוֹי לְקִבְלִי לְמֶעְבַּדְהוֹן וּקְיָמוֹי לָא אַעְדֵי מֶנִי:


For all his judgments *are revealed* in my sight, *to do them;* and his covenants I will not remove⁸⁸ from me.

footnotes:
⁸⁸ or, he has not removed


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 18:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:24)


וַהֲוֵיתִי שְׁלִים בִּדְחַלְתֵּיהּ וַהֲוֵיתִי נָטִיר נַפְשִׁי מֵחוֹבִין:


And I was⁸⁹ blameless in *fear of* him; and I kept *my soul* from sins.

footnotes:
⁸⁹ I will be


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 18:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:25)


וּפְרַע יְיָ לִי הֵיךְ זְכוּתִי כִּבְרִירוּת אַיְדִי קֳדָם מֵימְרֵיהּ:


And the LORD rewarded me according to my merit; according to the cleanness of my hands in the presence of his *word.*


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 18:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:26)


עִם אַבְרָהָם דְאִשְׁתַּכַּח חֲסִיד קֳדָמָךְ אַסְגֵיתָא לְמֶעְבַּד חִסְדָא עִם זַרְעֵיהּ דְיִצְחָק דַהֲוָה שְׁלִים בִּדְחַלְתָּךְ אַשְׁלֶמְתָּא מֵימַר רְעוּתָךְ עִמֵיהּ:


With *Abraham, who was found* pious *in your presence,* you showed much mercy; with *his seed, Isaac, who was* complete *in fear of you,* you completed *your favorable word.*⁹⁰

footnotes:
⁹⁰ cp. Vayiqra Rabba 11:5


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 18:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:27)


עִם יַעֲקֹב דַהֲוָה בְּרִיר קֳדָמָךְ בְּחַרְתָּא בְנוֹי מִן כָּל עַמְמַיָא וְאַפְרֵשְׁתָּא זַרְעֵיהּ מִן כָּל פְּסִילָא וְעִם פַּרְעֹה וְזַרְעֵיהּ וּמִצְרָאֵי דַחֲשִׁיבוּ מַחְשְׁבָן בִּישָׁן עַל עַמָךְ בַּלְבֵּלְתִּנוּן בְּמַחְשְׁבָתְהוֹן:


With *Jacob, who was* pure⁹¹ *in your presence,* you *chose his sons from all the Gentiles, and separated his seed from all that is unfit; but with Pharaoh and his seed, and the Egyptians who thought evil thoughts against your people⁹², you confounded them in their thoughts.*

footnotes:
⁹¹ walked in purity
⁹² + Israel


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 18:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:28)


מְטוּל דְאַנְתְּ יַת עַמָא בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל דַחֲשִׁיכִין בֵּינֵי עַמְמַיָא בְּגָלוּתָא אַנְתְּ עֲתִיד לְמִפְרַק וּבְמֵימְרָךְ אוּמַיָא תַקִיפַיָא דְמִתְגַבְּרִין עֲלֵיהוֹן תַּמְאִיךְ:


Because you are going to redeem the people, *the house of Israel, who are esteemed⁹³ among the peoples in exile;* and *by your word* you will abase *the mighty nations who prevail over them.*

footnotes:
⁹³ needy


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 18:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:29)


מְטוּל דְאַנְתְּ תַּנְהַר שְׁרָגָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל דְמִטַפְיָא בְגָלוּתָא דְאַנְתְּ הוּא מָרֵיהּ דִנְהוֹרָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל יְיָ אֱלָהִי יַפְקִנַנִי מֵחֶשְׁכָּא לִנְהוֹר יַחְמִנַנִי בְנִיחָמוּתִי דְעָלְמָא דַעֲתִיד לְמֵיתֵי לְצַדִיקַיָא:


For you will light the lamp *of Israel that was extinguished in the exile, for you are the lord of the light of Israel.* The LORD my God will bring me out of darkness into light⁹⁴; *he will show me his eternal consolation which is to come to the righteous.*

footnotes:
⁹⁴ + and shine before me


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 18:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:30)


מְטוּל דִבְמֵימְרָךְ אַסְגֵי מַשִׁרְיָן וּבְמֵימַר אֱלָהִי אֶכְבּוֹשׁ כְּרַכִּין תַּקִיפִין:


For by *your word* I will pass through armies; and by *the word* of my God I will *subdue mighty citadels.*


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 18:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:31)


אֱלָהָא דְכִוְנָן אָרְחָתֵיהּ אוֹרַיְיתָא דַייָ בְּחִירָא תְּרֵיס הוּא לְכָל דְמִתְרַחֲצִין עֲלוֹי:


God [is he] whose ways are true; the *Torah* of the LORD is *pure*; he is a shield to all who trust in him.⁹⁵

footnotes:
⁹⁵ his word


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 18:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:32)


אֲרוּם נִסָא וּפוּרְקָנָא דְתַעְבֵּיד לִמְשִׁיחָךְ וּלְשִׁיוּרֵי עַמָךְ דְיִשְׁתָּאֲרוּן יוֹדוּן כָּל עַמְמַיָא אוּמַיָא וְלִשָׁנַיָא וְיֵימְרוּן לֵית אֱלָהָא אֶלָא יְיָ אֲרוּם לֵית בַּר מִנָךְ וְעַמֵךְ יֵימְרוּן לֵית דְתַקִיף אֶלָא אֱלָהָנָא:


*For because of the miracle and deliverance that you will perform for your Messiah, and for the remnants of your people who will remain, all the Gentiles, nations, and tongues will confess and say,*There is no God but the LORD, *for there is none besides you; and your people⁹⁶ will say,* There is none *mighty* except our God.

footnotes:
⁹⁶ + the house of Israel


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 18:33

אֱלָהָא דִמְזָרֵז לִי קָמוּר בְּחֵילָא וּמְתַקֵן שְׁלִים אָרְחִי:


God, who girds on me *a belt* in strength, and makes blameless my way.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 18:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:34)


דִמְשַׁוֵי רִגְלַי קַלִילִין הֵיךְ אַיָלָתָא וְעַל בֵּית תּוּקְפִי יְקַימִנָנִי:


Who makes my feet⁹⁷ like hinds’; and he will sustain me in my *stronghold.*

footnotes:
⁹⁷ + swift


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 18:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:35)


מַלֵיף אַיְדֵי לְמֶעְבַּד קְרָבָא וּמַתְקֵיף הֵיךְ קֶשֶׁת כַּרְכּוֹמָא דַרְעָי:


Who teaches my hands to do battle, and who makes my arms as strong as a bronze bow.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 18:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:36)


וִיהַבְתְּ לִי תְקוֹף וּפוּרְקַן וִימִינָךְ תְּסֵיְיעֵנִי וּבְמֵימְרָךְ אַסְגַעְתַּנִי:


And you have given me *strength and* redemption; and your right hand will help me; and *by your word* you have multiplied me.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 18:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:37)


אַפְתֵּיתָ פְּסִיְעַתִי בְּאַתְרִי וְלָא אִזְדַעְזַא רְכוּבְתִּי:


You have broadened⁹⁸ my steps in my place, and my knee has not buckled.

footnotes:
⁹⁸ multiplied

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:38)


אֶרְדוֹף סָנְאַי וְשֵׁצִיתִנוּן וְלָא שְׁבוּת עַד דִגְמַרְתִּנוּן:


I will pursue my enemies; [now] have I destroyed them, and I did not return until I finished them off.⁹⁹

footnotes:

אֲגַמְרִנוּן וְלָא יָכִילוּ לְמֵקָם וּנְפַלוּ קְטִילִין תְּחוֹת פַּרְסָת רִגְלָי:


I will destroy them,¹⁰⁰ and they are unable to rise; and the *slain* have fallen under *the soles of* my feet.

וְזֵרַזְתָּא לִי חֵילָא הֵיךְ קָמוֹר לְמֶעְבַּד קְרָבָא תַּבַּרְתָּא עַמְמִין דְקַיְמִין לְאַבְאָשָׁא לִי תְחוֹתָי:


And you have girded me with strength as a belt to do battle; you have defeated beneath me *the Gentiles* who rise up *to do me harm.*

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:41)


וּבַעֲלֵי דְבָבִי תַּבַּרְתָּ קֳדָמַי יְהַבְתִּנוּן מְחַזְרֵי קְדַל וְסָנְאַי אֲשֵׁיצִנוּן:


And my foes *you have broken in my presence;* you have made them turn tail; [thus] my enemies I will destroy.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:42](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:42)


בָּעָן סָעִיד וְלֵית לְהוֹן פָּרִיק מַצְלָן קֳדָם יְיָ וְלָא מְקַבֵּל צְלוֹתְהוֹן:


They seek help, but they have no redeemer; *they pray in* the presence of the LORD, but he does not *accept their prayer.*

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:43](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/18:43)


וְדוֹשַׁשְׁתִּינוּן הֵיךְ גַרְגִשְׁתָּא דְאַרְעָא עַל אַנְפֵּי זַעְפָּא וְהֵיךְ סְיַן אַשְׁקָקֵי בַעֲטֵית אִנוּן:


I have crushed¹⁰¹ them like *clods* of earth before the storm-wind; and like the mud of the streets I have trodden them.

תְּשֵׁזְבִנַנִי מִפְּלֻגַת עַמְמַיָא תְּנַטְרִינַנִי בְמֵיזְרֵי מַזְיָן בְּרֵישׁ עַמְמַיָא עַמָא דְלָא חַכִּימַת יִפְלְחוּנַנִי:


You will deliver me from the discords of the Gentiles; you will keep me¹⁰² *by my destiny a benefactor* at the head of the Gentiles; a people that I did not know shall worship me.

לְשֵׁמַע אִידִין יִשְׁתַּמְעוּן לִי בְּנֵי עַמְמַיָא יְכַדְבוּן קֳדָמַי:

The LORD lives, and blessed is *the mighty one; for from his presence strength and redemption are given to me;* and exalted is God, *the strength of* my redemption.

אֱלָהָא דְעָבֵד פוּרְעֲנוּתָא לִי וּמְתַבַּר עַמְמַיָא דְקַיְמִין לְאַבְאָשָׁא לִי תְחוֹתִי:


It is God who works retribution for me, and *defeats* beneath me the Gentiles *who arise to do me harm.*

מְשֵׁזְבִי מִבַּעֲלֵי דְבָבִי לְחוֹד עַל דְקָיְימִין לְאַבְאָשָׁא לִי תִּגְבְּרִנַנִי מִן גוֹג וּמַשִׁרְיָת עַמְמִין דַחֲטוֹפִין עִמְהוֹן תְּשֵׁזְבִנַנִי:


He delivers¹⁰⁴ me from my foes; indeed against those who arise *to do me harm* you will make me prevail; you will deliver me from *Gog and the armies of* rapacious Gentiles *with him.*

מְטוּל הֵיכְנָא אוֹדֶה קֳדָמָךְ בֵּינֵי עַמְמַיָא יְיָ וְלִשְׁמָךְ אֲזַמֵר תּוּשְׁבַּחְתָּא:


Because of this, I will give praise in your presence among the Gentiles, O LORD; and I will sing *praises* to your name.

He works abundant redemption with his king, and shows favor to his anointed, to David and his seed forever.

אֲמַר אֱלָהָא לְמָה אַתּוּן טָפְזִין טוּרַיָא לֵית רַעֲוָתִי לְמִתַּן אוֹרַיְתָא עַל טוּרַיָא גֵיוְתָנִין מְבַסְרָנַיָא הָא טוּר סִינַי דְמַכִּיךְ רַגֵג מֵימְרָא דַייָ לְאַשְׁרָאָה עֲלוֹי שְׁכִינְתֵּיהּ לְחוֹד בִּשְׁמֵי שְׁמַיָא יְיָ יִשְׁרֵי לְעָלְמָא:


Said God: **Why are you leaping, mountains?** It is not My will to give the Torah upon **proud** scornful **mountains**! This **Mount** Sinai, which is humble; the word of the LORD **desires to dwell upon it;** even His presence in the heavens of heavens, **the LORD will dwell forever.**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 68:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/68:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/68:18)


אַרְתִּיכִין דֶאֱלָהָא תְּרֵין רִבְבָן דְנוּר דְלִיק תְּרֵין אַלְפִין דְאַנְגְלַיָא מַדְבְּרִין לְהוֹן שְׁכִנְתָּא דַייָ שְׁרָת עֲלֵיהוֹן עַל טוּרָא דְסִינָי בִּקְדוּשָׁה:


**The chariot**s **of God**are**double myriads** of burning fire, **two thousand** angels leading them, the presence of **the LORD** dwells **upon them** upon Mount **Sinai in holiness.**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 68:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/68:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/68:19)


סְלֵקְתָּא לִרְקִיעַ (מֹשֶׁה נְבִיָא) שְׁבִיתָא שִׁבְיְתָא (אַלֵפְתָּא פִּתְגָמֵי אוֹרַיְתָא) יְהַבְתָּא לְהוֹן מַתְּנָן לִבְנֵי נָשָׁא וּבְרַם סַרְבָּנַיָא דִי מִתְגַיְירִין וְתָיְבִין בִּתְתוּבָא שְׁרָת עֲלֵיהוֹן שְׁכִינַת יְקָרָא דַייָ אֱלֹהִים:


**You ascended to the** firmament, Moses the prophet, **You captured captives**, You taught words of Torah, gave **gifts to humans; even on the stubborn** who convert and turn in repentance, the glorious Presence of the LORD **God will dwell**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 68:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/68:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/68:20)


בְּרִיךְ יְיָ עַל כָּל יוֹמָא וְיוֹמָא טְעֵן לָנָא מוֹסִיף פִּקוּדַיָא עֲלַוֵי פִּקוּדַיָא תַּקִיפָא דִי הוּא פֻרְקָנָנָא וּסְעִידָנָא לְעָלְמִין:


**Blessed be the LORD** each and **every day, who loads us up,** adding commandments upon commandments; the mighty one who is **our redeemer and helper forever.**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 68:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/68:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/68:21)


אֱלָהָא לָנָא תְקוֹף וּפוּרְקָן וּמִן קֳדָם יְיָ מִתְגָרִין בְּרַשִׁיעַיָא מוֹתָא וּמַפְקָנוּת נִשְׁמָתָא בְּסַרְנוֹקָא:


**The God is for us** might and **redemption** from before **the LORD, death** and loss of breath via suffocation **is incited** against the wicked.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 68:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/68:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/68:22)


בְּרַם אֱלָהָא יִתְּבַר רֵישׁ בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבוֹי יְנַתֵּר שְׂעַר גְבַר דִי מִתְהַלֵךְ בְּחוֹבוֹ:


**But God will shatter the heads of His enemies,**making **the hair** fall out from the man who **walks about in his guilt.**

צַדִיקַיָא דְמִיתוּ וְאִתְאֲכַלוּ מִן חֵיוַת בְּרָא אֲמַר יְיָ מִכּוּתְנָן אֲתֵיב אֲתֵיב צַדִיקַיָא דִי יִשְׁתְּנִיקוּ בִּמְצוּלְתֵּיהּ דְיַמָא:


The righteous who died and have been eaten by wild beasts—**said the LORD—from Kutnan**Note **I will return; I will return** the righteous who have drowned in **the depths of the sea.**.

מִן בִּגְלַל דְיֶחֱמוּן פּוּרְעֲנוּת רַשִׁיעָא יִטְמְשׁוּן רַגְלֵיהוֹן בְּדַם קַטִילִין לִישָׁן חֵיוָת בָּרָא מִן תַּרְבֵּיהוֹן יִדְהֲנוּן מִן בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבָא מִנְהוֹן יִסְבְּעוּן:


**So that** they will see the punishment of the wicked, **dipping** their **feet in the blood**of the slain; **the tongue of** the wild beast will become fat off the fat of **their enemies, some of them** sated.

חֲמוֹן בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל הֲלִיכוֹת שְׁכִינְתָּךְ עַל יַמָא אֱלָהָא אָמְרִין הֲלִיכָת אֱלָהָא מְלַךְ כָּל עַלְמָא בִקְדוּשָׁא:


The house of Israel **saw the paths** of Your presence by the sea, **God**, saying, **"The paths of God, sovereign**over all the earth**in holiness!"**

אַקְדִימוּ וַאֲמַרוּ שִׁירְתָא בָּתַר מֹשֶׁה וְאַהֲרֹן דִמְנַגְנִין קֳדָמֵיהוֹן בִּמְצַע נְשַׁיָא צַדִיקָתָא דִי עִם מִרְיָם מְתוֹפְפִין:


**They rose** and sang **songs after** Moses and Aaron who were **making melody** before them, **among** the righteous women who were with Miriam **timbreling**.

בֵּינֵי כְנִישַׁיָא בָּרִיכוּ אֱלָהָא רוֹמְמוּ יְיָ עוֹבְרַיָא בִּמְעֵי אִמְהוֹן זַרְעָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל:


Among the **assemblies, bless God,** exalt **the LORD** fetuses in their mothers' bellies, seed of **Israel**!

תַּמָן בִּנְיָמִין זְעֵיר בְּשִׁבְטַיָא דְמִן שֵׁירוּיָא נְחַת לְיַמָא מְטוּל הֵיכְנָא קַבֵּל מַלְכוּתָא מִן שֵׁירוּיָא וּבַתְרֵיהוֹן נְחַתוּ רַבְרְבֵי יְהוּדָה רְגַמוּ יַתְהוֹן שִׁבְטַיָא אַבְנִין וְקַבִּילוּ רַבָּנוּתָא בַּתְרֵיהוֹן רַבָּנֵי זְבוּלֻן הֲווֹן תַּגָרֵיהוֹן רַבָּנֵי נַפְתָּלִי הֲווֹן גִבָּרֵהוֹן:


**Their Benjamin, youngest** of the tribes, who of their camp first went into the sea, on account of which he received kingship, after which went down **the nobles of Judah** who the tribes **stoned** with stones, and they received rule after them; **the nobles of Zebulun** were their merchants, the **the nobles of Naphtali** were their warriors.

פַּקֵד אֱלָהָא עוּשְׁנָךְ אִתְעַשַׁן אֱלָהָא שְׁרֵי בְּבֵית מַקְדְשָׁא דְנָן דַעֲבַדְתָּ לָנָא:


**God commanded your strength; be strong, God,** dwelling in Your Temple **that You made for us!**

מִן הֵיכְלָךְ תְּקַבֵּל קוּרְבָּנַיָא עַל יְרוּשְׁלֶם שְׁכִנְתָּךְ שָׁרְיָא מִפַּנִקְטֵיהוֹן לָךְ יַיְתוּן מַלְכַיָא קוּרְבָּנַיָא:


**From Your palace** receive sacrifices, **upon Jerusalem** Your presence dwells; **to You kings will** give sacrifices.

נְזוֹף בְּמַשִׁרְיַת חַיָבִין תְּרַע יַתְהוֹן הֵיךְ קַנְיָא כְּנִישַׁת גִבָּרִין דְמִתְרַחֲצִין בְּעֶגְלַיָא טַעֲוַת עַמַיָא רְעוּתֵיהּ בְּעַמָא דִי מִתְעַסְקִין בִּרְעוּתָא בְּאוֹרַיְתָא דִזְקִיקָא מִן סִימָא בַּדַר עַמַיָא דִלְאַגָחָא קְרָבָא צָבִין:


**Rebuke** the camps of sinners, break them down like **reeds,** the assembly of the **warriors** that trust in **calves**, in the idols of the nations! His favor is for the people that **willingly** engage in the Torah, which is **purer than silver. Scatter the nations that desire to wage battle!**

Let the children of Ḥam, the Uthmanitesnote, **come from Egypt** to be converted; the children of **Kush will run** to spread their **hands** in prayer before **God**.

**Kingdoms of the earth,**praise before the LORD; praise **the LORD forever.**

**Awesome is God from Your sanctuary**-house, mighty one **of Israel; He gives strength and migty to**His **people; bless God!**

צְלוֹתָא דְצַלֵי מֹשֶׁה נְבִיָא דַייָ כַּד חָבוּ עַמָא בֵית יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּמַדְבְּרָא עֲנֵי וְכֵן אָמַר יְיָ דְמַדוֹר בֵּית שְׁכִנְתָּךְ בִּשְׁמַיָא אַתְּ הֲוֵיתָא לָנָא סָעֵיד בְּכָל דָר וָדָר:


**The prayer** that **Moses** the prophet of the LORD prayed when the people, the house of Israel, sinned in the wilderness. He raised his voice and thus he said: **O LORD, the dwelling** of whose presence is in heaven, **you have been for us** a helper **in every generation.**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 90:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/90:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/90:2)


כַּד אִתְגְלֵי קֳדָמָךְ דְעַמָךְ עֲתִידִין לְמֵחוֹב אַתְקֵנְתָּא תְּתוּבָא עַד דְלָא טוּרַיָא אִתְנַטַלוּ וְאִתְבְּרִיאַת אַרְעָא וְיָתְבֵי תֵבֵל מִן עַלְמָא הָדֵין עַד עַלְמָא דְאָתֵי אַתְּ הוּא אֱלָהָא:


When it was manifest in your presence that your people were going to sin, you established repentance; **before ever the mountains were** lifted up **and the earth and the world**’s inhabitants **created, and from**this**age to the age**to come,**you are God.**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 90:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/90:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/90:3)


תְּתֵיב בַּר נַשׁ עַל חוֹבֵיהּ עַד מוֹתָא וַאֲמַרֵת תּוּבוּ בְנֵי נָשָׁא:


**You will return a son of man to**death because of his sin; **and**[yet]**you have said, “Repent, O sons of men.”**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 90:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/90:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/90:4)


אֲרוּם אֲלַף שְׁנִין בְּעֵינָךְ מִתְחַשְׁבִין קֳדָמָךְ הֵיךְ יוֹמָא דְאִתְמָלֵי אֲרוּם יֶעְבַּר וְהֵיךְ מַטְרַת לֵילְיָא:


**For a thousand years in your eyes**are considered in your presence**like a yesterday, for it will pass; and**like**a watch in the night.**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 90:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/90:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/90:5)


וְאִין לָא תָיְבִין תֵּיתֵי עֲלֵיהוֹן מוֹתָא הֵיךְ דְמָכִין יְהוֹן וּלְעָלְמָא דְאָתִי הֵיךְ עִסְבָּא דְמִתְפָּרְכָא יִתְחַלְפוּן:


And if they do not repent, death will come upon them, **they will be** as those who are **sleeping;** and in the age to come, **they will disappear like**crumbling **grass.**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 90:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/90:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/90:6)


דַמְיָן עוֹבָדֵיהוֹן הֵיךְ עִסְבָּא דִי בְצַפְרָא יְנִיץ וְיַסְגֵי לְרַמְשָׁא מִתְמוֹלֵל וּמִתְיַבֵּשׁ מִן חוּרְבָּא:


Their deeds are like grass that**in the morning will spring up and** multiply; **in the evening it fades and dries up** from the heat.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 90:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/90:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/90:7)


אֲרוּם אִשְׁתֵּיצָנָא מִן תּוּקְפָךְ וּמִן רוּגְזָךְ אִתְבַּהֵלְנָא:


**For we have been destroyed by your**harshness,**and by your anger¹ we have been terrified.**

footnotes:
¹ alternate Targum: rage


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 90:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/90:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/90:8)


שַׁוִיתָא חוֹבָנָא לְקִבְלָךְ עַוְיַת טַלְיוּתָנָא קָבֵל נְהוֹר אַפָּךְ:


**You have set our sins in front of you,**the iniquities of**our youth**before **the light of your face.**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 90:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/90:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/90:9)


אֲרוּם כָּל יוֹמָנָא אִתְפְּנִיאוּ קֳדָמָךְ בְּרוּגְזָךְ שֵׁצֵינָא יוֹמֵי חַיָנָא הֵיךְ הֲבֵל פּוּמָא דְסִתְוָא:


**For all our days**have been removed from your presence **in your anger; we have completed**the days of our lives **like** a vapor of the mouth in winter.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 90:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/90:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/90:10)


יוֹמֵי שְׁנוֹתָנָא בְּעָלְמָא הָדֵין שׁוּבְעִין שְׁנִין מִתְאַלְמִין וְאִין בִּגְבוּרְתָּא תַּמְנָן שְׁנִין וְסוּגְעֵיהוֹן לֵיאוּת וּשְׁקַר לְחַיָבַיָא אֲרוּם עֲדוּ בְּסַרְהוֹבְיָא וְטָיְסִין לְצַפְרָא:


**The days of our years** in this age**are seventy years,**quickly passing;²**and if** [one is]**in strength, eighty years; but most of them are toil and deceit** for the guilty,**for they pass in haste and fly away** to the morning.³

footnotes:
² Translation uncertain.
³ alternate Targum: dust.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 90:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/90:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/90:11)


מַן הוּא דְיָדַע לְאַתְבָא עוּשְׁנָא דְתוּקְפָךְ אֱלָהֵין צַדִיקַיָא דְדָחֲלִין מִנָךְ מְשַׁדְכִין רוּגְזָךְ:


**Who is he who knows**how to turn back **the force of your harshness?**– except the righteous, **who fear you,**appease **your anger.**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 90:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/90:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/90:12)


לְמִמְנֵי יוֹמָנָא כֵּן יִתְכַּוֵן לְהוֹדַע בְּרַם נְבִיָא דְלִבֵּיהּ מַבַּע חוּכְמָתָא:


Who **is right to teach us to number our days,**except **the prophet, whose heart**pours forth **wisdom?**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 90:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/90:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/90:13)


תּוּב יְיָ עַד אֵימָתַי תְסַגְפִנָנָא וְתוּב מִן בִּישְׁתָּא דִי מַלֵלְתָּא לְמֶעְבַּד לְעַבְדָךְ:


**Turn, O LORD – how long**will you afflict us? – **and**turn from the harm that you commanded to do **to your servants.⁴**

footnotes:
⁴ alternate Targum: people.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 90:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/90:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/90:14)


שַׂבַּע יָתָנָא לְעַלְמָא דִמְתִיל לְצַפְרָא טוּבָךְ וּנְרַנֵן וְנֶחְדֵי בְּכוּלְהוֹן יוֹמָנָא:


**Satisfy us with your goodness in** the age that is likened to a**morning, and we will rejoice and be glad in all our days.**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 90:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/90:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/90:15)


שַׂמַח יָתָנָא הֵיךְ יוֹמִין דְסַגִפְתָּנָא הֵיךְ שְׁנַיָא דַחֲמֵינָא בִּישְׁתָּא:


**Gladden us like the days that you afflicted us,**like**the years that we saw harm.**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 90:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/90:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/90:16)


יִתְחֲמֵי לְעַבְדָךְ עוֹבָדֵי נִסָךְ וְשִׁבְהוֹרָךְ עַל בְּנֵיהוֹן:


**Let the works**of your miracles**appear to your servants, and let your splendor be upon their sons.**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 90:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/90:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/90:17)


וִיהֵי בְסִימוּתָא דְגַן עֵדֶן מִן קֳדָם יְיָ עֲלָנָא וְעוֹבָדֵי יְדֵינָא מִנֵיהּ יִתַּקְנוּן:


**And may the pleasantness**of the Garden of Eden **be upon us**from the presence **of the LORD our God, and the works of our hands will be established by him.⁵**

footnotes:
⁵ some manuscripts conclude: and may the works of our hands flourish; he has firmly set the words of Torah upon us.



## Chapter 91

###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 91:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/91:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/91:1)


דְאַשְׁרֵי שְׁכִנְתֵּיהּ בְּרָזָא עִלָאָה בִּטְלַל עֲנָנֵי יְקָרָא דְשַׁדַי יְבִית:


**He who** makes his presence abide**in secret is the Most High; he will lodge in the shadow of** the clouds of the glory of**Shaddai.**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 91:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/91:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/91:2)


אֲמַר דָוִד אֵימַר לַיְיָ רוּחְצָנִי וּכְרַךְ תּוּקְפִי אֱלָהִי אָכְלֵי בְּמֵימְרֵיהּ:


David said: **“I will say to the LORD, ‘My confidence and my** strong**fortress’; my God, I will trust in****his word.”**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 91:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/91:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/91:3)


אֲרוּם הוּא יִפְצִנָךְ שְׁלֹמֹה בְּרִי מִפַּחָא וְתִקְלָא מִמוֹתָא וְאִתְרְגוּשְׁתָּא:


**For he will deliver you,** Solomon my son,**from the snare** and**the obstacle, from death**and**confusion.**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 91:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/91:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/91:4)


בִּטְלַל שְׁכִנְתֵּיהּ יַטְלַל עֲלָךְ וּתְחוֹת טְלַל יְקָרֵיהּ תִּתְרְחֵץ תְּרִיסָא וַעֲגִילָא הֵימְנוּתֵיהּ:


**With** the shelter of his presence**he will shelter you, and you will be confident** under the shelter of his glory; **his faithfulness is a shield and buckler.**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 91:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/91:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/91:5)


לָא תִדְחַל מִן דְלוּחָא דְמַזִיקֵי דְאָזְלִין בְּלֵילְיָא מִן גִרְרָא דְמַלְאָךְ מוֹתָא דְשָׁדֵי בִימָמָא:


**Be not afraid of the terror of** demons who walk at**night, of the arrow** of the angel of death that he looses**during the day;**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 91:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/91:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/91:6)


מִן מוּתָא דִי בְקִבְלָא מְהַלֵךְ מִסִיעַת שֵׁידִין דִמְחַבְּלִין בְּטִיהֲרָא:


**Of the** death**that walks in darkness, of** the band of demons**that attacks at noon.**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 91:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/91:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/91:7)


תַּדְכַּר שְׁמָא דְקוּדְשָׁא יִפְּלוּן מִן סְטַר שְׂמָאלָךְ אַלְפָא וְרִבְבוּתָא מִן יְמִינָךְ לְוָתָךְ לָא יִקְרְבוּן לְמִנְזָק:


You will invoke the holy name;**a thousand will fall at your** left**side, and ten thousand at your right; they will not come near you** to do harm.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 91:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/91:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/91:8)


לְחוֹד בְּעַיְנָךְ תְּהֵי מִסְתַּכַּל וְהֵיךְ מִתְגַמְרִין רַשִׁיעֵי תֶחֱמֵי:


**Only with your eyes you will watch, and you will see the wicked**as they are destroyed.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 91:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/91:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/91:9)


עֲנֵי שְׁלֹמֹה וְכֵן אֲמַר אֲרוּם אַנְתְּ הוּא יְיָ רוּחֲצָנִי בִּמְדוֹר עִלָאָה שַׁוֵיתָא בֵּית שְׁכִנְתָּךְ:


Solomon answered and said: **“For you are my confidence, O LORD;** in the**highest** dwelling place**you have placed the house** of your presence.”


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 91:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/91:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/91:10)


אֲתִיב מָרֵי עַלְמָא וְכֵן אֲמַר לָא תְאָרַע לָךְ בִּישְׁתָּא וּמַכְתָּשָׁא וּמַזִיקַיָא לָא יִקְרְבוּן בְּמַשְׁכְּנָיִךְ:


The lord of the world responded and thus he said: **“No harm shall happen to you; and no plague** or demon**shall come near to your tents.”**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 91:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/91:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/91:11)


אֲרוּם מַלְאֲכוֹי יְפַקֵד עֲלָךְ לְמִטְרִינָךְ בְּכָל אוֹרְחָתָךְ:


**For he will command his angels concerning you to guard you in all your ways.**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 91:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/91:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/91:12)


עַל תּוּקְפֵיהוֹן יִטְלוּנָךְ דִלְמָא תַתְקֵל בְּיִצְרָא בִישָׁא דִמְתִיל לְאַבְנֵי רִגְלָךְ:


**They will lift you up by their** strength,**lest you stumble on** the evil impulse, which is likened to**the stones at your feet.**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 91:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/91:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/91:13)

**Because he has taken pleasure in my word, and I will deliver him; I will exalt him because he knows my name.**

## Chapter 104

###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 104:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:1)


בָּרִיכִי נַפְשִׁי יַת שְׁמָא דַייָ יְיָ אֱלָהִי אִתְגַדַלְתָּא לַחֲדָא שְׁבָחָא וְשִׁבְהוֹרָא אַלְבִּישְׁתָּא:


**Bless, O my soul,**the name of**the LORD. O LORD my God, you are greatly exalted; you have put on praise and splendor.**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 104:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:2)


דְמִתְעַטֵף נְהוֹרָא הֵיךְ סְדִינָא דִמְתַּח שְׁמַיָא הֵיךְ יְרִיעֲתָּא:


**Who wraps himself in light like a sheet, who stretches out the heavens like a curtain.**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 104:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:3)


דְטָלֵל בְּמַיָא הֵיךְ בִּנְיָנָא דְכָשׁוּרֵי אַכְסַדְרוֹי דְשַׁוֵי עֲנָנִין אַרְתִיכֵיהּ עַל עֲנָנִין קְלִילִין הֵיךְ כַּנְפֵי נִשְׁרָא:


**Who covers his chambers with water**like a building with beams;**who placed his chariot**, as it were,**upon swift clouds; who goes on the wings of**an eagle.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 104:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:4)


דַעֲבַד אִזְגַדוֹ סַרְהוֹבִין הֵיךְ רוּחָא שַׁמָשׁוֹי תַּקִיפִין הֵיךְ אֶשָׁא מְצַלְהֲבָא:


**Who made his messengers**as swift as**wind; his servants,**as strong as**burning fire.**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 104:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:5)


דִי מְיַסַד אַרְעָא עֲלַוֵי בְּסִיסָהּ דְלָא תָזוּעַ לְעַלְמֵי עָלְמִין:


**Who lays the foundation of the earth upon its base, so that it will not shake for ages upon ages.**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 104:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:6)


תְהוֹמָא כַּד בִּלְבוּשָׁא חֲפִיתָהּ וּמְבַזְעִין עַל טוּרַיָא וְקָיְמִין מַיָא:


**You have covered over the abyss as with a garment; and the waters**split**on the mountains,**and**endure.**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 104:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:7)


מִן מְזוֹפִיתָךְ יַעַרְקוּן לְמִגַד מִן קָל אַכְלָיוּתָךְ יִתְבַּהֲלוּן לְמִשְׁפָּךְ:


**At your rebuke, they will flee,**flowing down;**at the sound of your shout, they will be frightened,**pouring themselves out.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 104:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:8)


יְסַלְקוּן מִן תְּהוֹמָא לְטוּרַיָא וְנָחֲתִין בְּבִקְעֲתָא לַאֲתַר דְנָן דִיסַדְתָּא לְהוֹן:


**They will go up**from the abyss**to the mountains,**and**descend to the valleys, to this place**that**you founded for them.**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 104:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:9)


תְּחוּם שַׁוֵיתָא לְגַלְלֵי יַמָא דִי לָא יֶעְבְּרוּן דִי לָא יְתוּבוּן לְמֶחְפֵּי אַרְעָא:


**You have placed a boundary**for the waves of the sea**that they will not cross,**lest**they return to cover the earth.**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 104:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:10)


דִי מְשַׁלַח מַעֲיָנֵי בְּנַהֲרוּתָא בֵּינֵי טוּרַיָא מְהַלְכִין:


**Who releases springs into rivers; they flow between the mountains.**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 104:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:11)


מַשְׁקִין כָּל חֵיוַת בָּרָא יִתְבְּרוּן עֲרוֹדַיָא צָחוּתְהוֹן:


**They water all the wild animals; the asses will break their thirst.**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 104:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:12)


עִלַוֵיהוֹן צִפְּרֵי שְׁמַיָא יִשְׁרוּן מִבֵּינֵי זָאַזְיָא יִתְּנוּ קָל זְמָר:


**The birds of heaven will settle on them; they will give out a sound**of singing**from among the branches.**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 104:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:13)


דְמַשְׁקֵי טוּרַיָא מִן בֵּית גִנְזֵי עִלָאִי דִי לֵיהּ מִן עוֹבָדָיִךְ תִּשְׂבַּע אַרְעָא:


Who**waters the mountains from his**upper treasury;**the earth will be satisfied with the fruit of your deeds.**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 104:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:14)


דִמְרַכֵּי עִסְבָּא לִבְעִירֵי וְיַרְקֵי לְפוּלְחָנָא דְבַר נָשָׁא לְמִפַּק לַחְמָא מִן אַרְעָא:


Who**makes grass grow for beasts, and herbs for the cultivation of**the son of**man, that bread may come forth from the earth;**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 104:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:15)


וְחַמְרָא דְמֶחְדֵי לִבָּא דְבַר נָשָׁא לְאַנְהָרָא אַפַּיָא מִן מִשְׁחָא וְלַחְמָא לִבָּא דְבַר נָשָׁא יִסְעָד:


**And wine**that**gladdens the heart of**the son of**man, to make the face shine by oil; and bread will support the heart of**the son of**man.**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 104:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:16)


שָׂבְעִין אִילָנַיָא דִי בְרָא יְיָ אַרְזֵי לִבְנָן דִי נְצִיב:


**The trees**that**the LORD**created**are satisfied, the cedars of Lebanon that he planted:**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 104:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:17)


דִי תַמָן צִפְּרַיָא עָבְדִין שַׁרְכְּפִין וָוְתָא בְרָאתִי בֵּית מְדוֹרָה:


**Where the birds make nests; the stork’s dwelling is in the cypresses.**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 104:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:18)


טוּרַיָא רָמַיָא לְיַעֲלֵי כֵּיפֵי רוּחֲצָן לְטַפְזַיָא:


**The high mountains are for the wild goats; the rocks are security for the conies.**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 104:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:19)


עֲבַד סִיהֲרָא לְמִימְנֵי בֵיהּ זִמְנַיָא שִׁמְשָׁא יָדַע עִידָן טְמוֹעֵיהּ:


**He made the moon**to calculate**times**by; the sun knows the time of**his setting.**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 104:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:20)


תְּשַׁוֵי חֲשׁוֹךְ וַהֲוֵי לֵילְיָא בֵּיהּ רָחֲשִׁין כָּל חֵיוַת חוּרְשָׁא:


**You will make darkness and it will be night; in it all the beasts of the forest creep about.**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 104:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:21)


בְּנֵי אַרְיְוָן אָכְלִין לְמִשְׁכַּח מְזוֹנָא וּלְמִבְעֵי מִן אֱלָהָא מֵיכַלְהוֹן:


**The**offspring of**lions roar**to find**food, and to seek their sustenance from God.**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 104:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:22)


תִּדְנַח שִׁמְשָׁא מִתְכַּנְשִׁין וּלְבֵית מְדוֹרְהוֹן רָבְעִין:


**The sun will shine, they gather together; and they lay down in their dwelling place.**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 104:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:23)


יִפּוֹק בַּר נָשָׁא לְעוֹבָדֵיהּ וּלְפוּלְחָנֵיהּ עַד עֵרוּבֵי רַמְשָׁא:


**A**son of**man will go forth to his work and to his cultivation, until**the sunset of**evening.**


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 104:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/104:24)


מָה סַגִיעִין הִנוּן עוֹבָדָךְ יְיָ כּוּלְהוֹן בְּחוּכְמְתָא עֲבַדְתָּא מַלְיָא אַרְעָא קִנְיָנָךְ:


**How many are your works, O LORD! You have made all of them in wisdom; the earth is full of your possessions.**

**Blessed are you**from before **the LORD, Maker of heaven and earth.**

## Chapter 120

###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 120:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/120:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/120:1)


שִׁירָא דְאִתְאַמַר עַל מַסוּקִין דִתְהוֹמָא קֳדָם יְיָ כַּד עַיִקִ לִי צְלֵיתִי וְקַבֵּל צְלוֹתִי:


The song that is said on the ascents from the abyss. Before *Adonai*, when I was distressed, I prayed, and He received my prayer.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 120:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/120:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/120:2)


יְיָ פְּצֵי נַפְשִי מִסְפְוָתָא דְשִׁקְרָא מִן לִישָׁנָא נְכִילְתָּא:


*Adonai* saves my soul from the lips of falsehood, from the deceitful tongue.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 120:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/120:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/120:3)


מָא יְהֵיב לָךְ מַלְשִׁינָא וּמָא יוֹסִיף לָךְ אָכִיל קוּרְצִי לִישָׁנָא נְכִילְתָא:


What will it give you, badmouther, and what benefit to you, informant, is the deceitful tongue?


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 120:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/120:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/120:4)


גִרְרִין דְגִבָּרָא שְׁנוּנִין הֵיךְ בַּרְקִין מִלְעֵיל עִם גוּמְרֵי רִתְמִין דְדָלְקִין בִּגְהִנָם מִלְרַע:


Sharp arrows of a warrior, like lightning above, with coals of broom wood that burn in hell below.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 120:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/120:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/120:5)


וַי לִי אַרוּם אִתּוֹתְבִית עִם אוֹסָאֵי שְׁרֵית עִם מַשְׁכְּנֵיהוֹן דַעֲרָבָאֵי:


Woe is me, since I am made to live with the Asians, to dwell in the tents of the Arabs!


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 120:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/120:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/120:6)


יַתִּיר מִנְהוֹן שְׁרָת נַפְשִׁי עִם אֱדוֹם סָנֵי שְׁלָם:


Too long has my soul lived with Edom, hater of peace.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 120:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/120:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/120:7)


אֲנָא שְׁלָם וַאֲרוּם אֲצַלֵי הִנוּן לִקְרָבָא:


I am peace, and since I pray they go to war.



## Chapter 121

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/121:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/121:1)


שִׁירָא דְאִתְאַמֵר עַל מַסוּקִין דִתְהוֹמָא אֶזְקוֹף עֵינַי לְטוּרַיָא מִן הָאן יֵיתֵי סַעֲדִי:


The song that is said on the ascents from the abyss. I raise up my eyes to the mountains — from where comes my help?

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/121:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/121:2)

My help is from before *Adonai*, who made the heaven and earth.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 121:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/121:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/121:3)


לָא יִתֵּן לְאִתְמוֹטָטָא רִגְלָךְ לָא נָיֵם נָטְרָךְ:


He will not make your feet totter, unsleeping is your Guardian.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 121:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/121:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/121:4)


הָא לָא נָיֵם וְלָא יִדְמוּךְ נָטַר יִשְׂרָאֵל:


Behold — unsleeping and unresting is the guardian of Israel.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 121:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/121:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/121:5)


יְיָ יִנְטְרִנָךְ יְיָ יְטַלֵל עֲלָךְ עַל סְטַר יְמִינָךְ:


*Adonai* will guard you. *Adonai* will cast shade upon you on account of the mezuzah that is affixed to the right side of your entrance.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 121:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/121:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/121:6)


יֵימָם כַּד שְׁלִיט שִׁמְשָׁא לָא יִמְחוּנָךְ צַפְרִירֵי וְלֵילֵי כַּד שַׁלִיט סִיהֲרָא בְּלֵילְיָא:


In the day, when the sun rules, the zephyrs will not strike you; nor the lilin, when the moon rules in the night.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 121:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/121:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/121:7)


מֵימְרָא דַייָ יִנְטְרִנָךְ מִכָּל בִּישׁ יִנְטוֹר יַת נַפְשָׁךְ:


The word of *Adonai* will guard you from all evil, will guard your soul.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 121:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/121:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/121:8)


יְיָ יִנְטוֹר מַפְקָךְ לִפְרַגְמַטְיָא וּמַעֲלָךְ לְמֶעֱסַק בְּאוֹרַיְתָא מִן הַשְׁתָּא וְעַד עַלְמָא:


*Adonai* will guard your going out to business and your going in to engage in Torah from now and until forever.



## Chapter 122

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/122:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/122:1)


שִׁירָא דְאִתְאֲמַר עַל מַסוּקִין דִתְהוֹמָא חֲדֵיתִי בְּאָמְרִין לִי בֵּית מַקְדְשָׁא דַייָ נֵיזֵיל:


The song that is said on the ascents from the abyss. I rejoiced when they were saying to me, "To the Holy Temple of *Adonai* let us go!"

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/122:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/122:2)

Our feet were standing in your gates, Jerusalem!

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/122:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/122:3)

Jerusalem that is built in the firmament is like the city that is joined with it as one on earth.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 122:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/122:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/122:4)

That there went up the tribes, tribes of *Adonai*, testifying to Israel that His Presence dwells among them, to make them go to thank the name of *Adonai*.

Because there were installed thrones in Jerusalem: thrones in the Holy Temple of the kings of the house of David.


###### Aramaic Targum to Psalms 122:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/122:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/122:6)


שְׁאִילוּ בִּשְׁלָמָא דִירוּשְׁלֶם יֵתְבוּן בְּשַׁלְוָתָא רְחִימָיִךְ:


Ask peace for Jerusalem, may those who love you dwell in security.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/122:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Aramaic%20Targum%20to%20Psalms/r/122:7)
